naṉṉūl mūlamum caṅkara namaccivāyaruriyum

    • ((nāṉkāvatu iṭiccolliyal))

((nūṟ ciṟappuppāyiram))

NA_ca_0

malartali yulakiṉ malkiru ḷakala
ilakoḷi parappi yāviyum viḷakkum
parutiyi ṉorutā ṉāki mutalī
ṟoppaḷa vāci muṉivikan tuyarnta
5 aṟputa mūrttitaṉ ṉalartaru taṉmiyiṉ
maṉaviru ḷiriya māṇporuṇ muḻuvatum
muṉivaṟa varuḷiya mūvaṟu moḻiyuḷum
kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭam
eṉunāṉ kelliyi ṉiruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ
10 arumporu ḷintiyu miyāvaru muṇarat
tokivaki viriyiṟ ṟarukeṉat tuṉṉār
ikalaṟa nūṟi yirunila muḻuvatum
taṉateṉak kōlit taṉmata vāraṇam
ticitoṟu niṟuviya tiṟaluṟu tolcīrk
15 karuṅkaḻal veṇkuṭik kārnikar vaṇkit
tiruntiya ceṅkōṟ cīya kaṅkaṉ
aruṅkali vinōta ṉamarā paraṇaṉ
moḻintaṉa ṉāka muṉṉōr nūliṉ
vaḻiyē naṉṉūṟ peyariṉ vakuttaṉaṉ
20 poṉmatiṟ caṉakic caṉmati muṉiyaruḷ
paṉṉaruñ ciṟappiṟ pavaṇanti
eṉṉu nāmat tiruntavat tōṉē.

eṉpatu pāyiram.
malartali yulakiṟ palanū lāyntu
ceyvatun tavirvatum peṟuvatu muṟuvatum
uyvatu maṟiyē ṉoruporu ḷāka
naṉṉeṟi piṟaḻā naṟṟavat tōrpeṟum
5 taṉṉaṭit tāmari tanteṉi yāṇṭa
tiruvā vaṭutuṟi tēcika ṉākiya
karuṇiyaṅ kaṭaliyeṉ kaṇṇiviṭṭakalāc
cuvāmi nāta kuravaṉi yaṉutiṉam
maṉamoḻi meykaḷil vaṇaṅkiyava ṉaruḷāṟ
10 poṉmali yeṉavip puvipukaḻ perumi
maṉṉiya vūṟṟu malimaru tappaṉ
muttamiḻp pulamiyu muṟiyara curimiyum
ittalat teytiya viṟimaka ṉātaliṉ
naṉṉūṟ kurinī naviyaṟac ceytu
15 paṉṉūṟ pulavarmuṉ pakartiyeṉ ṟiyampaliṉ
naṉṉā valarmuka nakinā ṇāmē
eṉṉā liyaṉṟavi yiyaṟṟumin nūluḷ,

ippāyiram eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, ciṟappup pāyirat tilakkaṇamāka mēl vakukkappaṭum ākkiyōṉ peyar mutaliyaṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(itaṉ poruḷ), malartali yulakiṉ paranta iṭattiyuṭiya ulakattiṉkaṇṇē, malku iruḷ akala - niṟinta kaṇṇiruḷ keṭa, ilaku oḷi parappi - viḷaṅkum katiri virittu, yāviyum viḷakkum - kaṭpoṟikku viṭayamākiya uruvamaṉittiṉiyum kāṭṭum, parutiyiṉ - cūriyaṉippōla, orutāṉāki - ulakuk kellām tāṉ oru mutalēyāki, mutal īṟu oppu aḷavu āci muṉivu ikantu uyarnta - tōṟṟamum oṭukkamum uvamiyum aḷavum viruppum veṟuppumākiya avaṟṟi iyalpākavē nīṅki niṟṟalāl talivaṉākiya aṟputa mūrtti - ñāṉamē tirumēṉiyākavuṭiyāṉ, taṉ alartaru taṉmiyiṉ - taṉatu virinta taṉmiyākiya karuṇiyiṉālē, maṉaviruḷ iriya - uyirkaḷiṉ maṉattiruḷākiya avitti keṭa, māṇporuḷ muḻuvatum - māṭcimippaṭṭa aṟamporuḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṉum nāṉku poruḷiyum, muṉivu aṟa aruḷiya - viruppuṭaṉ aruḷicceyta, mūvaṟu moḻiyuḷum - patiṉeṇṉilattu moḻikaḷuḷḷum, kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭameṉum nāṉku elliyiṉ iruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ - innāṉkelliyiṉiyuṭiya nilattu moḻiyākiya periya tamiḻeṉṉuṅkaṭaluḷ, arum poruḷintiyum - avvaṟa mutaṟ poruḷ nāṉkiyumuṇartaṟkuk karuviyāy arumiyavākiya eḻuttuc coṟporuḷ yāppu aṇiyeṉṉum aintu poruḷiyum, yāvarumuṇara - puttiyāṉ valiyōrēyaṉṟi eḷiyōru muṇara, toki vaki viriyiṉ tarukeṉa - immuttiṟamum oruṅkamittuk kūṟappaṭum yāppiṉāṟ pāṭit tarukaveṉa, tuṉṉār ikal aṟa nūṟi - pakivaratu pakimikeṭa avarit tuṇittu, irunilamuḻuvatum taṉateṉak kōli - periya pūmiyaṉittiṉiyum taṉṉu#aiṭuyatākap paṟṟikkoṇṭu, taṉmatavāraṇam ticitoṟum niṟuviya tiṟaluṟu tolcīr - taṉ matayāṉikaḷi eṭṭuttikkilum ticikkaḷiṟukaḷ pōla niṟuttiya veṟṟi mikunta taṉṉaḷavēyaṉṟit taṉtāti mūtāti mutaliyōrit toṭṭu varuṅ kīrttiyiṉiyum, karuṅkaḻal - perumi poruntiya vīrakkaḻaliyum, veṇkuṭi - veṇkoṟṟak kuṭiyiṉiyum, kārnikar vaṇki mēkattippōlak kimmāṟu karutātu koṭukkum vaṇkiyiṉiyum, tiruntiya ceṅkōl - kōṭāta ceṅkōliṉiyumuṭiya, cīyakaṅkaṉ - ciṅkam pōṉṟa kaṅkaṉ, aruṅkali vinōtaṉ - ariyanūlkaḷi ārāytalē poḻutu pōkkum viḷiyāṭṭākavuṭiyāṉ, amarāparaṇaṉ - taṉṉakattu viḻuppuṇpaṭa amar ceytaliyē āparaṇamākavuṭiyāṉ, moḻintaṉaṉāka - coṉṉāṉāka, muṉṉōr nūliṉ vaḻiyē - tollāciriyar nūliṉvaḻiyē naṉṉūṟ peyariṉ vakuttaṉaṉ - naṉṉūleṉṉum peyariṉāṟ ceytaṉaṉ, poṉ matil caṉaki - poṉmatil cūḻnta caṉakāpurattuḷ irukkum, caṉmatimuṉiyaruḷ - caṉmati muṉivaṉ peṟṟa, paṉṉaruñciṟappiṟ pavaṇanti eṉṉum nāmattu - collutaṟkariya ñāṉavoḻukkac ciṟappiṉiyum pavaṇantiyeṉṉum peyariṉiyum uṭiya, iruntavattōṉ - periya tavattiṉiyuṭiyōṉeṉṟavāṟu.

itaṉuḷ pavaṇantiyeṉa ākkiyōṉ peyarum, muṉṉōr nūliṉvaḻiyēyeṉa vaḻiyum, nāṉkelliyeṉa elliyum, naṉṉūleṉa nūṟpeyarum, tokivakiviriyeṉa yāppum, arumporuḷinteṉa nutaliyaporuḷum, cīyakaṅkaṉ tarukeṉa moḻintaṉaṉeṉak kēṭpōrum, māṇporuṇmuḻuvatumeṉap payaṉum, cīyakaṅkaṉeṉavē avaṉ kālattuppāṭi avaṉ avikkaḷattu ēṟṟiyateṉak kālamum kaḷaṉum, yāvarumuṇara veṉak kāraṇamum uṇarttiyavāṟu kāṇka.

tarukaveṉak kēṭpōrik kēṭpōreṉṉātu innūli araṅkēṟṟuḻik kēṭpōrik kēṭpōreṉpārumuḷar; kaḷaṉeṉṟatu kēṭpōraviyiyātaliṉ atu kūṟiyatu kūṟalāmeṉka. nūlik kūṟuvōrkkum kēṭpōrkkum payaṉ aṟam mutaliya nāṉkumeṉpatu tōṉṟa "nāṟporuṭpayattōṭu" (cū. 4) eṉṟum, "aṟamporu ḷiṉpamvī ṭaṭitaṉūṟ payaṉē" (cū. 10) eṉṟum kūṟuvataṟintum yāvaru muṇartalip payaṉeṉavum kūṟupa. aṉṟiyum, yāvaru muṇara vēṇṭuvār. vēṭṭaṉavum avvaṟamutaliya payaṉāṉmēyātalāṉum atu poruntāteṉka.

nālvakiyāppiṉuḷ tokiviriyāppeṉa oṉṟu pōntataṉṟit tokivakiviriyeṉap pōntatilliyālōveṉiṉ, naṭuniṉṟa vaki piṉṉiṉṟa viriyinōkkiṟ ṟokiyākavum, muṉṉiṉṟa tokiyinōkkiṉ viriyākavu maṭaṅkutaliṉ itu tokiviri yāppeṉṟataṉpāṟ paṭumeṉka. eṉavē toki viriyeṉa iraṇṭāyvariṉum marattiṉatu parāriyiṉiṉṟum kavaṭu kōṭu kompu vaḷār palavāy oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭar paṭṭeḻuntu niṟṟalpōl tokiyiṉiṉṟum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpaṭap pakukkappaṭṭup palavāy variṉum tokiviriyāppōyāmeṉka.

nutaliyaporuḷ arumporuḷinteṉa uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, innūluṭ kūṟiya poruḷ yāppu aṇiyeṉṉum mūṉṟatikāraṅkaḷum akkālattuḷḷaṉa pōlum. ippāyiram oru nūṟkurittātaliṟ ciṟappuppāyiramāmeṉka.

((uricciṟappuppāyiram))

((viruttam))

(1) naṉṉūluṭ karuttulakō raṟiyavuri ceykaveṉa narēntra ciṅkam
teṉṉūṟṟu malimarutap paṉpukalap poruḷviḷaṅkac ceytāṉ pāril
ennūṟku meḻuttoṭucoṟ poruḷaṟicaṅ karanamacci vāya ṉeṉṉum
paṉṉūṟcen tamiḻppulavaṉ civacikā maṇinellip patiyi ṉāṉē.

(2) akattiyantol kāppiyamē mutaliyamuṉ ṉūlkaḷpala vāyntu muppāl
pakutta tiru vaḷḷuvarē mutaṉñāṉa nūlkaḷelām pakalcey vāṉpōl
ikattiluri ceyavalacaṅ karanamacci vāyaṉeṉum iyaṟpēr peṟṟa
takippulava ṉaṉṉūluk kurivakutta toruviyappō tamiḻval lōrē.

(3) muṉṉūṟku malayamuṉi taṉṉūṟkum puviyiṭattu mutiyō rāṟcol
ennūṟkun tamiḻkkaṭalu ḷiṉiyiyaṟṟu nūṟkumvaki itaṉāṟ kāṭṭi
naṉṉūṟku viritturicey tāṉṟikan tattaḷavum naṭattuṅ kīrttit
teṉṉūṟṟu malimarutap paṉcolaccaṅ karanamacci vāyaṉ ṟāṉē.

((potuppāyiram))

((pāyirattiṉ peyarkaḷ))

NA_ca_1

mukavuri patika maṇinturi nūṉmukam
puṟavuri tanturi puṉinturi pāyiram.

eṉpatu cūttiram. eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, pāyirattiṟku varum kāraṇakkuṟikaḷ uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pāyirattiṉilakkaṇaṅkaḷi muṉṉuṇarntallatu nūlkaḷic ceytalum avaṟṟi ītalum ēṟṟalum muṭiyāmi karuti muṉṉurittaliṉ mukavuriyeṉṟum, "patikak kiḷavi palvakip poruḷit, tokuti yākac colluta ṟāṉē" eṉpa vākaliṉ mēlvakukkum aintupotuvum patiṉoru ciṟappumākiya palvakipporuḷiyum tokuttuc collutaliṟ patikameṉṟum, nūliṉatu perumi mutaliya viḷaṅka aṇinturittaliṉ aṇinturi yeṉṟum, mukavuriyeṉṟatupōla nūṉmukameṉṟum, nūlnutaliyaporu ḷallaṉavaṟṟi urittaliṟ puṟavuriyeṉṟum, nūṟkuḷḷa nutaliya poruḷallaṉavaṟṟi ataṟkut tanturittaliṉ tanturi yeṉṟum, aṇinturi yeṉṟāṟ pōlap puṉinturiyeṉṟum pāyirattiṟkup peyarām e-ṟu.

pāyirameṉpatu varalāṟu. pāyiraṅkūṟap pukuntavar nikaṇṭupōla ataṉ peyarvikaṟpaṅkaḷik kūṟiyatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, ippāyiram patikamākiya puṟavuriyāyt tanturikkappaṭuvatēṉum nūṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta aṇiyāy muṉ urikkappaṭuvateṉpatu ikkāraṇakkuṟikaḷāṉ viḷaṅkutaliṉeṉka.

((pāyira vaki.))

NA_ca_2

pāyiram potucciṟap peṉaviru pāṟṟē.

e-ṉ, mēṟ pāyirameṉṟataṉi vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟpāyirameṉṟatu, potuppāyiramum ciṟappuppāyiramumeṉa iruvakiyiṉiyuṭittu e-ṟu.

((potuppāyirattilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_3

nūlē nuvalvō ṉuvalun tiṟaṉē
koḷvōṉ kōṭaṟ kūṟṟā mintum
ellā nūṟku mivipotup pāyiram.

e-ṉ, iruvakip pāyirattuṭ potuppāyiratti virittuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūliṉatu varalāṟum āciriyaṉatu varalāṟum avvāciriyaṉ nūli māṇākkaṟkuc collutaliṉ varalāṟum māṇākkaṉatu varalāṟum avaṉ kēṭṭaliṉ varalāṟum eṉṉum aintum ellā nūṟkum āmātaliṉ, ivvintu varalāṟṟiyum viḷaṅka uṇarttuvatu potuppāyiramām e-ṟu.

tiṟaṉ - kūṟṟu: eṉṟatu varalāṟṟiyātaliṉ ataṉi ēṉiyavaṟṟuḷḷum piritturittām, āmeṉpataṉi ellā nūṟkumāmeṉak kūṭṭuka.
"īvōṉ ṟaṉmi yīta liyaṟki
koḷvōṉ ṟaṉmi kōṭaṉ marapeṉa
īriraṇ ṭeṉpa potuviṉ ṟokiyē"

eṉpārumuḷarālōveṉiṉ, pāyiraṅkūṟutal nūlkaṭkaṉṟip piṟavaṟṟiṟkaṉṟē? aṅṅaṉamātaliṉ, nūlkaḷiṉ varalāṟoṉṟumē kūṟavēṇṭum; ataṉōṭu āciriyaṉ varalāṟu mutaliya nāṉkiṉiyum uṭaṉkūṟutal varalāṟṟu muṟimiyiṉ vārātār nūlkaḷik kaṟpikkavum kaṟkavum pukiṉ,
"poypaṭu moṉṟō puṉipūṇuṅ kiyaṟiyāp
pēti viṉimēṟ koḷiṉ" (kuṟaḷ, 836)

eṉṟavāṟēyāy nūlkaḷarumiyum perumiyum keṭṭup payaṉpaṭāvāmeṉpatu karutiyeṉka.

((1. nūliṉ varalāṟu))

NA_ca_4

nūli ṉiyalpē nuvali ṉōriru
pāyiran tōṟṟi mummiyi ṉoṉṟāy
nāṟporuṭ payattō ṭeḻumatan taḻuvi
aiyiru kuṟṟamu makaṟṟiyam māṭciyō
ṭeṇṇāṉ kuttiyi ṉōttup paṭalam
eṉṉu muṟuppiṉiṟ cūttiraṅ kāṇṭiki
virutti yākum vikaṟpanaṭi peṟumē.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya aintaṉuḷ nūliṉatu varalāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya aintaṉuḷ nūliṉatu varalāṟṟic colliṉ, iruvakip pāyirattiyum muṉṉuṭittātal mutalāka iṅṅaṉam vikaṟpitta patiṉoru vikaṟpanaṭikaḷip peṟṟu varum e-ṟu.

((nūlkaḷiṉ vaki))

NA_ca_5

mutalvaḻi cārpeṉa nūṉmūṉ ṟākum.

e-ṉ, nūliṉatu varalāṟṟit tokuttuk kūṟiṉār, avaṟṟuḷ ōriru pāyirattiṟku vaki pōntu kiṭantamiyiṉ ataṉi yoḻittu ēṉiyavaṟṟi vakuttuk kūṟuvāṉ toṭaṅki mummi yiṉoṉṟāy" (cū. 4) eṉṟataṉi vakuttu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mutaṉūl vaḻinūl cārpunūleṉa mūṉṟu kūṟṟatām nūl e-ṟu.

((mutaṉūl))

NA_ca_6

avaṟṟuḷ,
viṉiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ
muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum.

e-ṉ, nūl mūṉṟaṉuḷ mutaṉūlāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) viṉiyiṉ nīkki viḷakkappaṭum aṟiviṉiyuṭiya uyirkaṭku viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉiyuṭiya mutalvaṉ ātikkaṇṇē ceytatu yātu? atu mutaṉūlām e-ṟu.

((vaḻinūl))

NA_ca_7

muṉṉōr nūliṉ muṭiporuṅ kottup
piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅ kūṟi
aḻiyā marapiṉatu vaḻinū lākum.

e-ṉ, vaḻinūlāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) tollāciriyar nūlkaḷiṉ poruṇmuṭipu muḻuvatu mottu vaḻi nūl ceyvōṉ mutaṉūluḷatākavum tāṉ vaḻinūl ceytaṟkuk kāraṇamāka vēṇṭiya vikaṟpaṅkaḷiyum uṭaṉkūṟi avvikaṟpaṅkaḷ uṇarvuṭiyōr palarkkum oppa muṭintamiyiṉ aḻiyātu ulakattu niṉṟu nilavum marapiṉi uṭiyatu vaḻinūlām e-ṟu.

piṉṉōṉvēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅkūṟalāvatu, paḻiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum vaḻuvala (cū. 462) veṉṟu avaṟṟi iṟantatu vilakkal, etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttikaḷāṉ vilakkiyum pōṟṟiyuṅ kūṟutal mutaliyaṉa.

`muṉivaṉkaṇṭatu mutaṉūlākum' (cū. 6) eṉa orumiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. īṇṭu muṉṉōr nūleṉap paṉmiyāṟ kūṟiyatu. iṟivaṉūliyum avaṉaruḷ vaḻippaṭṭut tattamarapiṉvarum kuravar palar nūliyum taḻīikkōṭaṟkeṉka. eṉavē mutaṉūl māttiriyāy niṟpatu iṟivaṉūlum vaḻinūl māttiriyāy niṟpatu iṟuti nūlumaṉṟi iṭiniṟkum nūlkaḷellām oruvaṟku mintaṉāyiṉāṉ maṟṟoruvaṟkut tantiyiṉāṟ pōla mutaṉūlāyum vaḻinūlāyum niṟkumeṉpatu peṟṟām.

((cārpu nūl))

NA_ca_8

iruvar nūṟku moruciṟi toṭaṅkit
tiripuvē ṟuṭiyatu puṭinū lākum.

e-ṉ, cārpunūlāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mutaṉūl vaḻinūleṉṉum irutiṟattu nūlkaṭkum poruṇmuṭipu orupuṭiyottu oḻintaṉavellām ovvāmiyuṭiyatu cārpunūlām e-ṟu.

eṉavē, mutaṉūṟku vaḻinūlum cārpunūlum oruvaṟku mintaṉum marumāṉum pōlumeṉka. orunūl, taṉakku vaḻi nūlinōkkiṉ mutaṉūlākavum, mutanūlinōkkiṉ vaḻi nūlākavum, ayaṉūli nōkkiṟ cārpunūlākavum niṟkumāyiṉum immuttiṟattiṉuḷ oru tiṟattiṉkaṇ maṟṟoru tiṟamiṉmiyiṉ mummiyiṉoṉṟā yeṉṟār.

((vaḻinūl cārpunūṟ ciṟappuviti))

((veṇpā))

NA_ca_9

muṉṉōr moḻi poruḷē yaṉṟi yavarmoḻiyum
poṉṉēpōṟ pōṟṟuva meṉpataṟkum - muṉṉōriṉ
vēṟunūl ceytumeṉu mēṟkōḷil leṉpataṟkum
kūṟupaḻañ cūttirattiṉ kōḷ.

e-ṉ, vaḻinūṟkum cārpunūṟkum eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu. muṉṉōr nūliṉ poruṇmuṭipu muḻuvatum oppavum orupuṭiyoppavum kūṟuvataṉṟi annūṟ cūttiraṅkaḷiyum orōvaḻi eṭuttuk kūṟuka veṉṟaliṉ.

(i-ḷ.) muṉṉōr moḻinta poruḷiyē yaṉṟi avar moḻiyiṉiyum poṉpōlap pōṟṟikkoḷva meṉpataṟku ilacciṉiyākavum muṉṉōr nūliyē kūṟātu annūliṉiṉṟum vaḻinūl cārpunūl ceytōmāyiṉum āciriyavacaṉaṅkaḷi orōvaḻi eṭuttu uṭaṉ kūṟutal vaḻi nūṟkum cārpunūṟkum ilakkaṇamātaliṉ innūlakattu āciriyavacaṉamilli yeṉak kuṟṟaṅ kūṟuvārātaliṉ akkuṟṟam oḻitaṟkākavum paḻañ cūttirattiṉ kōḷik kūṟu e-ṟu.

āciriyavacaṉameṉiṉum mēṟkōḷeṉiṉum paḻañcūttirattiṉ kōḷeṉiṉum okkum. muṉ pōṟṟuvam, ceytumeṉa uḷappāṭṭut taṉmippaṉmimuṟṟākavum, piṉṉark kūṟeṉa muṉṉiliyorumi yēvalākavum kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, vaḻinūlum cārpunūlum ceyvōr palar kuḻīi oruvark koruvar kūṟuṅ kūṟṟāka ic cūttirañ ceyyappaṭṭateṉka.

((nāṟporuṭpayaṉ))

NA_ca_10

aṟamporu ḷiṉpamvī ṭaṭitaṉūṟ payaṉē.

e-ṉ, nāṟporuṭ payattōṭeṉṟataṉi vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) aṟamutaliya nāṉkiṉiyum ōtiṉōraṭital nūlāṉ varum payaṉām e-ṟu.

((eḻu matam))

NA_ca_11

eḻuvaki matamē yuṭaṉpaṭaṉ maṟuttal
piṟartam matamēṟ koṇṭu kaḷivē
tāa ṉāṭṭit taṉātu niṟuppē
iruvar māṟukō ḷorutali tuṇivē
piṟarnūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭa lēṉip
piṟitoṭu paṭāaṉ ṟaṉmataṅ koḷalē.

e-ṉ, eḻumatameṉṟataṉi vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēl eḻuvaki matameṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa iṅṅaṉam piṟarmatatti uṭaṉpaṭal mutaliyavāka vitantaṉavām e-ṟu.

nūltaḻuviya mataṅkaḷ palavāyiṉum talimipaṟṟi matamēḻeṉpatu nūl vaḻakkāmeṉka.

kuṟṟam pattu, aḻuku pattu, utti muppattiraṇ ṭeṉpaṉavum aṉṉa.

ivveḻuvaki mataṅkaṭkum patapporuḷōṭu utāraṇam viḷaṅka innūluḷ vantuḻi vantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka.

((kuṟṟam pattu))

NA_ca_12

kuṉṟak kūṟaṉ mikipaṭak kūṟal
kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟal
uccoṟ puṇarttaṉ mayaṅka vittal
veṟṟeṉat toṭuttaṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittal
ceṉṟutēyn tiṟuta ṉiṉṟupaya ṉiṉmi
eṉṟivi yīriṅ kuṟṟa nūṟkē.

e-ṉ, pattuk kuṟṟameṉpataṉi vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūṟkup pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷāvaṉa kuṉṟak kūṟal mutaliyavikaḷām e-ṟu.

nūlkaḷ ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷ cārātu varutal kāṇka.

((aḻaku pattu))

NA_ca_13

curuṅkac collal viḷaṅka vittal
naviṉṟōrk kiṉimi naṉmoḻi puṇarttal
ōci yuṭimi yāḻamuṭit tātal
muṟiyiṉ vippē yulakamali yāmi
viḻumiyatu payattal viḷaṅkutā raṇatta
tākuta ṉūliṟ kaḻakeṉum pattē.

e-ṉ, pattaḻakeṉṟataṉi vakattuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūliṟku aḻakeṉṟu collappaṭum pattāvaṉa curuṅkac collal mutaliyavām e-ṟu.

nūlkaḷ ippattaḻakōṭuṅ kūṭivarutal kāṇka.

((utti muppattiraṇṭu))

NA_ca_14

nutalip pukuta lōttumuṟi vippē
tokuttuc cuṭṭal vakuttuk kāṭṭal
muṭittuk kāṭṭaṉ muṭiviṭaṅ kūṟal
tāṉeṭuttu moḻital piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal.
5 coṟporuḷ viritta ṟoṭarccoṟ puṇarttal
iraṭṭuṟa moḻita lētuviṉ muṭittal
oppiṉ muṭittaṉ māṭṭeṟin toḻukal
iṟantatu vilakka letiratu pōṟṟal
muṉmoḻintu kōṭal piṉṉatu niṟuttal
10 vikaṟpattiṉ muṭittaṉ muṭintatu muṭittal
urittu meṉṟa lurittā meṉṟal
orutali tuṇita leṭuttuk kāṭṭal
eṭutta moḻiyi ṉeyta vittal
iṉṉa talla tituveṉa moḻital
15 eñciya colli ṉeytak kūṟal
piṟanūṉ muṭinnatu tāṉuṭaṉ paṭutal
taṉkuṟi vaḻakka mikaveṭut turittal
colliṉ muṭivi ṉapporuṇ muṭittal
oṉṟiṉa muṭitta ṟaṉṉiṉa muṭittal
20 uyttuṇara vippeṉa vuttiyeṇ ṇāṉkē.

e-ṉ, muppattiraṇṭu uttiyeṉṟataṉi vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) muppattiraṇṭuttiyāvaṉa nutalippukutal mutaliyavām e-ṟu.

ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṉamuṭittal taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpatu ōr utti; uyttuṇaravippeṉpataṉi uttikku aṭiyākki uyttuṇaravippeṉum utti eṇṇāṉkēyeṉap pāṭamōti ataṉi iraṇṭuttiyākkiṉumamiyum.

ivvuttikaṭkup patapporuḷōṭu utāraṇam viḷaṅka innūluḷ vantuḻi vantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka. matattiṉuḷḷum aḻakiṉuḷḷum varuvaṉa cilavaṟṟi uttiyuḷḷum kūṟiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, koḷki vakiyāṉ matameṉṟum ciṟappu vakiyāṉ aḻakeṉṟum immatam aḻaku mutaliyavellām putti nuṭpattu amiyum vakiyāṉ uttiyeṉṟum kūṟappaṭumeṉka. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, matam aḻakellāvaṟṟiyum uttiyiṉpāṟpaṭuttik kūṟātu cilavaṟṟik kūṟiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, varampiṉṟi varum uttiyuḷ talimipaṟṟik kūṟum muppattiraṇṭil vantaṉa kūṟiṉāreṉka.

((utti iṉṉateṉpatu))

NA_ca_15

nūṟporuḷ vaḻakkoṭu vāyppak kāṭṭi
ēṟpuḻi yaṟintitaṟ kivvaki yāmeṉat
takumvaki celuttuta ṟantira vutti.

e-ṉ, mēl tokuttum vakuttuṅ kūṟiya uttikku ilakkaṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ōr ilakkaṇanūlāl uṇarttappaṭum poruḷi nūlvaḻakkōṭum ulakavaḻakkōṭum porunta uṇartti, apporuḷi maṟṟōr ilakkiya nūṟkaṇṇum ēṟkumiṭam aṟintu ivviṭattiṟku iḵtāmeṉakkarutit takkavakiyākac celuttutal tantiravuttiyām e-ṟu.

tantirameṉpatu nūl uttiyāṉ amikkum poruḷi uttiyeṉṟatu ākupeyar.

((ōttilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_16

nēriṉa maṇiyi niralpaṭa vittāṅ
kōriṉap poruḷi yoruvaḻi vippa
tōtteṉa moḻipa vuyarmoḻip pulavar.

e-ṉ, iruvakiyuṟuppiṉuḷ ōttuṟuppāmāṟuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) oru cātiyāyuḷḷa maṇikaḷi muṟiyē patittāṟ pōla orucātiyāyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷi oruvaḻippaṭak kūṟuvatu ōttuṟuppāmeṉac colluvar, uyirkku uṟuti payakkum meymmoḻikaḷiyuṭiya pulavar e-ṟu.

nērtal - oṉṟupaṭutal. ivvāṟu varutal innūluṟuppiṉuṭ kāṇka.

((paṭalavilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_17

oruneṟi yiṉṟi viraviya poruḷāṟ
potumoḻi toṭari ṉatupaṭala mākum.

e-ṉ, paṭalavuṟuppāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) oruvaḻippaṭātu virāyaporuḷoṭu poruntip pala poruḷi yuṇarttum potuc coṟkaḷ orōvaḻiyiṉṟit toṭarntuvariṉ atu paṭalavuṟuppām e-ṟu.

paṭalavuṟuppiṉiyuṭiya kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉatu caritiyēyaṉṟi mali kaṭal nāṭu mutaliya palaporuṭ ṭiṟaṅkaḷum viravivarutalum pala poruḷiyuṇarttum potuc coṟkaḷ orōvaḻiyaṉṟit toṭarntuvarutalum kāṇka.

((cūttiravilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_18

cilavaki yeḻuttiṟ palvakip poruḷic
cevva ṉāṭiyiṟ ceṟittiṉitu viḷakkit
tiṭpa nuṭpañ ciṟantaṉa cūttiram.

e-ṉ, cūttiramāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ciṟiya kaṇṇāṭiyiṟ periya yākki mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ cāyi cevvākac ceṟintu iṉitāka viḷaṅkiṉāṟpōlac cevvākac ceṟintu iṉitāka viḷaṅkac cilvaki yeḻuttukkaḷāṉ iyaṉṟa yāppiṉkaṭ palavakippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷic cevvākac ceṟittu iṉitāki viḷakki appalaporuṭṭiṇmiyum nuṇmiyum ciṟantuvaravaṉa cūttiraṅkaḷām e-ṟu.

tiṭpam - kuṟṟamiṉmiyiṉ alivaṟa niṟṟal. ivvāṟu varutal iccūttirattuḷḷuṅ kāṇka.

((cūttira nili))

NA_ca_19

āṟṟoḻuk karimā nōkkan tavaḷip
pāyttup paruntiṉvīḻ vaṉṉacūt tiranili.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarntu niṟkum nilikaḷ āṟṟoḻukku mutaliya nāṉkiṉiyum pōlum e-ṟu.

ivvāṟu niṟṟal innūluḷ vantuḻi vantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka.

((cūttira vaki))

NA_ca_20

piṇṭan tokivaki kuṟiyē ceyki
koṇṭiyal puṟaṉaṭik kūṟṟaṉa cūttiram.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) piṇṭameṉavum, tokiyeṉavum, vakiyeṉavum, kuṟiyeṉavum, ceykiyeṉavum ivaṟṟi alivaṟak koṇṭu ivaṟṟiṉ puṟattu aṭiyāy varum puṟaṉaṭiyeṉavum kūṟum kūṟṟiṉiyuṭiyaṉavām, mēṟkūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷ e-ṟu.

ivaṟṟuṭ piṇṭamāvaṉa: "paṉṉirupāṟ ṟatuvē" (cū. 57) eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattut tokipōlātu, "naṉkiyampuvaṉeḻuttē" (cū. 56) eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup potuppaṭavaruvaṉa. tokiyum vakiyum puṟaṉaṭiyum vantuḻivantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka. viri vakiyiṉpāṟ paṭutaliṟ kūṟārāyiṉar. kuṟiyāvaṉa: ivi uyir, ivi oṟṟu, ivi peyar, ivi viṉi eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu aṟitaṉ māttiriyāy varuvaṉa. kuṟiyeṉpatu aṟitaliyuṇarttiya mutaṉilit toḻiṟ peyar. ceykiyāvaṉa: patamuṉ vikutiyum patamu murupum puṇarum puṇarcci vitiyaṟintu aṅṅaṉamaṟitaṉ māttiriyāy nillātu avvāṟu vēṇṭuḻip puṇarttalic ceytalum, peyar viṉi mutaliya koḷḷum muṭipu vitiyaṟintu aṅṅaṉam aṟitaṉ māttiriyāy nillātu avvāṟu vēṇṭuḻi vēṇṭuḻi muṭittalicceytalu mutaliyaṉa. ippiṇṭam mutaliya āṟu peyarum apporuḷiyuṇarttuñ cūttiraṅkaṭku ākupeyarāy vantaṉa. ellāc cūttiraṅkaḷum piṇṭam, toki, vaki, puṟaṉaṭiyeṉṉum nāṉkaṉuḷ aṭaṅkumaṉṟō? aṅṅaṉamākak kuṟiyiṉiyum ceykiyiṉiyum vēṟōtiyatu, ellā nūluḷḷum collappaṭuvaṉa aṟivatum ceyvatumiṉṟi vēṟiṉmiyiṟ piṇṭamākkiyum tokuttum vakuttum puṟattaṭi koṭuttum kūṟappaṭuvaṉa kuṟiyum ceykiyumē yeṉpata tōṉṟaṟkeṉka. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟavē ellā nūluḷḷum varum ellāc cūttiraṅkaḷum kuṟiccūttiram ceykic cūttirameṉa iraṇṭā yaṭaṅkumeṉpatu peṟṟām. ivvāṟaṉṟik kuṟiyeṉpataṟkap peyarkaḷi yuṇarttum cūttirameṉṟum, ceyki yeṉpataṟku eḻuttuppuṇarcciyi yuṇarttum cūttira meṉṟum poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar. aṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiṉ, ēṉiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷik kūṟum cūttiraṅkaḷiyum vitantu kūṟavēṇṭum; avi iṉmiyāṉum peyarum puṇarcciyum ivvilakkaṇa nūṟkaṉṟip potuppāyiramāy ellā nūṟkum poruntāmiyāṉum atu poruḷaṉṟeṉka.

((uriyiṉ potuvilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_21

pāṭaṅ karuttē colvaki coṟporuḷ
tokutturi yutāraṇam viṉāviṭi vicēṭam
virivati kāran tuṇivu payaṉō
ṭāciriya vacaṉameṉ ṟīrē ḻuriyē.

e-ṉ, mēṟ kāṇṭikiyuri viruttiyuri yeṉṟār, avaṟṟi uṇarttuvāṉṟoṭaṅki, uriyiṉatu potu vilakkaṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ippatiṉāṉku vakiyāṉ urikkappaṭum nūṟku uri e-ṟu.

ivaṟṟuḷ, vicēṭamāvatu: cūttirattuṭ poruḷaṉṟi āṇṭikku vēṇṭuvaṉa tanturittal; virivāvatu: vēṟṟumi mutaliya tokku niṉṟaṉavaṟṟi virikkavēṇṭuḻi viritturittal; atikāramāvatu: eṭuttukkoṇṭa atikāram ituvātaliṉ iccūttirattu atikaritta poruḷ ituveṉa avvatikārattōṭu porunta urikkavēṇṭuḻi urittal; tuṇivāvatu: aiyuṟak kiṭantuḻi itaṟku ituvē poruḷeṉa urittal; ēṉiya poruḷ viḷaṅkik kiṭantaṉa.

ippatiṉāṉkum ivvuriyuḷ vantuḻi vantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka.

((kāṇṭikiyuri))

NA_ca_22

karuttup patapporuḷ kāṭṭu mūṉṟiṉum
avaṟṟoṭu viṉāviṭi yākka lāṉum
cūttirat tuṭporu ṭōṟṟuva kāṇṭiki.

e-ṉ, kāṇṭikiyuriyāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya patiṉāṉkiṉuṭ karutturiyum patapporuḷum utāraṇamumākiya mūṉṟaṉiyum urittalāṉum ammūṉṟaṉōṭu viṉā viṭiyeṉṉum iraṇṭaṉiyum kūṭṭiyurittalāṉum cūttirattuṭporuḷi viḷakkuvaṉa kāṇṭikiyurikaḷām e-ṟu.

((viruttiyuri))

NA_ca_23

cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyu māṇṭik
kiṉṟi yamiyā yāviyum viḷaṅkat
taṉṉuri yāṉum piṟarnū lāṉum
aiya makalaviṅ kāṇṭiki yuṟuppoṭu
meyyiṉi yeñcā ticippatu virutti.

e-ṉ, viruttiyuriyāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kāṇṭikiyuripōṟ cūttirattuṭporuḷ viḷakkutaṉ māttiriyiṉillātu avviṭaṅkaṭku iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaḷ yāviyum viḷaṅkat tāṉurikku muriyāṉum āciriyavacaṉaṅkaḷālum mēṟkūṟiya kāṇṭikiyuṟuppu aintaṉāṉum aiyantīrac curuṅkātu meymmipporuḷi viritturippatu viruttiyuriyām e-ṟu.

cūttirattuṭporuḷaṉṟiyu meṉṟamiyāṉ, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉāṉkuṟuppuṅ koṇṭatu viruttiyuri yeṉpatu peṟṟām.

((nūleṉṉum peyarkkāraṇam))

((veṇpā))

NA_ca_24

pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuva liḻiyākac
ceñcoṟ pulavaṉē cēyiḻiyā - eñcāta
kiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
miyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu.

e-ṉ, nūleṉṉum peyar varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūl muṭiyumāṟu ituvātaliṉ, nūleṉṟatu uvamavākupeyarām e-ṟu.

NA_ca_25

urattiṉ vaḷamperukki yuḷḷiya tīmip
purattiṉ vaḷamurukkip pollā - marattiṉ
kaṉakkōṭṭan tīrkkunū laḵtēpōṉ māntar
maṉakkōṭṭan tīrkkunūṉ māṇpu.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) annūlpōṟ ceppañceytalāṉum nūleṉṟatu uvamavāku peyarām e-ṟu.

purattiṉatu urattiṉ vaḷam perukki uḷḷiya tīmivaḷamurukkiyeṉak kūṭṭuka. puram - uṭampu; uram-neñcu. piṉpu tīmivaḷameṉṟamiyāṉ muṉpu nalvaḷameṉat tanturikka; nalvaḷam - aṟivu; tīmivaḷam - aṟiyāmi.

((2. āciriyariyalpu))

((nallāciriyar))

NA_ca_26

kulaṉaru ṭeyvaṅ koḷki mēṉmi
kalipayi ṟeḷivu kaṭṭuri vaṉmi
nilamali niṟikōṉ malarnikar māṭciyum
ulakiya laṟivō ṭuyarkuṇa miṉiyavum
amipava ṉūluri yāciri yaṉṉē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē āciriyaṉatu varalāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviyalpiṉiyuṭiyār kaṟpikkum āciriyarāvār e-ṟu.

NA_ca_27

terivarum perumiyun tiṇmiyum poṟiyum
paruva muyaṟci yaḷaviṟ payattalum
maruviya naṉṉila māṇpā kummē.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya uvamiyi virittu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nilattiṉatu māṭcimi ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_28

aḷakka lākā vaḷavum poruḷum
tuḷakka lākā niliyun tōṟṟamum
vaṟappiṉum vaḷantarum vaṇmiyu malikkē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) maliyiṉatu māṭcimi ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_29

aiyan tīrap poruḷi yuṇarttalum
meynnāṭu niliyu miku niṟi kōṟkē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) niṟikōliṉatu māṭcimi ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_30

maṅkala māki yiṉṟi yamiyā
tiyāvaru makiḻntu mēṟkoḷa melkip
poḻutiṉ mukamalar vuṭiyatu pūvē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) malariṉatu māṭcimi ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

ivvuvamikaḷiṉatu māṭcimippōlap poruḷkaḷiṉatu māṭcimiyiyum virittuk koḷka.

((āciriyarallātavar))

NA_ca_31

moḻikuṇa miṉmiyu miḻikuṇa viyalpum
aḻukkā ṟavāvañca macca māṭalum
kaḻaṟkuṭa maṭaṟpaṉi paruttik kuṇṭiki
muṭatteṅ koppeṉa muraṇkoḷ cintiyum
uṭiyō rilarā ciriyarā kutalē.

e-ṉ, āciriyarākār ivareṉa uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷiyuṭiyōr kaṟpikkum āciriyarākār e-ṟu.

NA_ca_32

peytamuṟi yaṉṟip piṟaḻavuṭaṉ ṟarum
ceyti kaḻaṟpey kuṭattiṉ cīrē.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya uvamiyi virittu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kaḻaṟpeyta kuṭattiṉ kuṟṟam ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamiyāyiṟṟu.

NA_ca_33

tāṉē tarakkoḷi ṉaṉṟit taṉpāl
mēvik koḷakkoṭā viṭattatu maṭaṟpaṉi.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) maṭaṟpaṉiyiṉ kuṟṟam ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_34

aritiṟ peyakkoṇ ṭapporu ṭāṉpiṟark
keḷitī villatu paruttik kuṇṭiki.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) paruttipeyta kuṇṭikiyiṉ kuṟṟam ituvātaliṉ ītu uvamiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_35

palvaki yutavi vaḻipaṭu paṇpiṉ
allōrk kaḷikku matumuṭat teṅkē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) muṭatteṅkiṉatu kuṟṟam ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

ivvuva mikaḷiṉ kuṟṟampōlap poruḷkaḷiṉ kuṟṟamum viritturittukkoḷka.

3. pāṭañcollutaliṉ ilakkaṇam

NA_ca_36

īta liyalpē yiyampuṅ kālik
kālamu miṭaṉum vālitiṉ nōkkic
ciṟantuḻi yiruntutaṉ ṟeyvam vāḻtti
urikkap paṭumporu ḷuḷḷat tamittu
viriyāṉ vekuḷāṉ virumpi mukamalarntu
koḷvōṉ koḷvaki yaṟintavaṉuḷaṅ koḷak
kōṭṭamiṉ maṉattiṉūl koṭutta leṉpa.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē āciriyaṉ kaṟpikkumāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kaṟpikkumāṟu ivvāṟeṉac colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

4. māṇākkar

NA_ca_37

taṉmaka ṉācāṉ makaṉē maṉmakaṉ
poruṇaṉi koṭuppōṉ vaḻipaṭu vōṉē
urikōḷa ḷāḷaṟ kurippatu nūlē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē māṇākkaṉatu varalāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvaṟuvarkkum collappaṭuvatu nūl e-ṟu.

((māṇākkar vaki))

NA_ca_38

aṉṉa māvē maṇṇoṭu kiḷiyē
illik kuṭamā ṭerumi neyyari
aṉṉar taliyiṭi kaṭimā ṇākkar.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya aṟuvaruḷ aṉṉamum pacuvum pōlvār talimāṇākkar: maṇṇum kiḷiyum pōlvār iṭimāṇākkar; illikkuṭamum āṭum erumiyum neyyariyum pōlvār kaṭimāṇākkar e-ṟu.

ivvuvamittiṟamum poruṭṭiṟamum viritturittukkoḷka.

((kaṟpikkappaṭātōr))

NA_ca_39

kaḷi maṭi māṉi kāmi kaḷvaṉ
piṇiya ṉēḻi piṇakkaṉ ciṉattaṉ
tuyilvōṉ mantaṉ ṟoṉṉūṟ kañcit
taṭumā ṟuḷattaṉ ṟaṟukaṇaṉ pāvi
paṭiṟaṉiṉ ṉōrkkup pakarār nūlē.

e-ṉ, māṇakkarākārivareṉa uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷi yuṭiyārkku nūlic collār āciriyar e-ṟu.

5. pāṭaṅkēṭkum muṟi

NA_ca_40

kōṭaṉ marapē kūṟuṅ kālip
poḻutoṭu ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭaṉ muṉiyāṉ
kuṇattoṭu paḻaki yavaṉkuṟip piṟcārn
tiruveṉa viruntu colleṉac collip
parukuva ṉaṉṉa vārvatta ṉākic
cittirap pāviyi ṉattaka vaṭaṅkic
cevivā yāka neñcukaḷa ṉākak
kēṭṭavi kēṭṭavi viṭātuḷat tamittup
pōveṉap pōta veṉmaṉār pulavar.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē māṇākkaṉ nūlik kaṟkumāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kaṟkumāṟu ivvāṟeṉac colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

((payiṟci))

NA_ca_41

nūlpayi liyalpē nuvaliṉ vaḻakkaṟital
pāṭam pōṟṟal kēṭṭavi niṉittal
ācāṟ cārntavi yamivarak kēṭṭal
ammāṇ puṭiyōr tammoṭu payiṟal
viṉātal viṉāyavi viṭutta leṉṟivi
kaṭaṉāk koḷiṉē maṭanaṉi yikakkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟu nūlip payiṉṟavarkku aṟiyāmi mikutiyum pōm e-ṟu.

NA_ca_42

orukuṟi kēṭpō ṉirukāṟ kēṭpiṉ
peruka nūliṟ piḻipā ṭilaṉē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) orukāṟ kēṭṭa tuṇiyāṉē amiyātu irukāṟ kēṭṭāṉāyiṉ annūliṉkaṭ piḻipāṭu mikutiyum ilaṉ e-ṟu.

NA_ca_43

mukkāṟ kēṭpiṉ muṟiyaṟin turikkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mukkāṟ kēṭpāṉāyiṉ āciriyaṉ kaṟpitta muṟiyaṟinturippaṉ e-ṟu.

NA_ca_44

ācā ṉuritta tamivarak koḷiṉum
kāṟkū ṟallatu paṟṟala ṉākum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) āciriyaṉ kaṟpittaṉavaṟṟit taṉ aṟiviṉkaṇ amiyak kaṟṟāṉāyiṉum pulamittiṟattiṟ kāṟkūṟallatu paṟṟāṉ e-ṟu.

NA_ca_45

avviṉi yāḷaroṭu payilvaki yorukāl
cevviti ṉurippa vavviru kālum
miyaṟu pulami māṇpuṭit tākum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) taṉpōliyaroṭu payilumvakiyāṟ kāṟkūṟum akkalviyit taṉ māṇākkarkkum avikkaḷattōrkkum uṇara viritturittalāṉ arikkūṟumākak kuṟṟamaṟṟa pulami nirampum e-ṟu.

((vaḻipāṭu))

NA_ca_46

aḻali ṉīṅkā ṉaṇukā ṉañci
niḻali ṉīṅkā ṉiṟinta neñcamō
ṭettiṟat tācā ṉuvakku mattiṟam
aṟattiṟ ṟiriyāp paṭarcci vaḻi pāṭē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟoḻukutal vaḻipāṭām e-ṟu.

((ciṟappupā yirattilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_47

ākkiyōṉ peyarē vaḻiyē yelli
nūṟpeyar yāppē nutaliya poruḷē
kēṭpōr payaṉō ṭāyeṇ poruḷum
vāyppak kāṭṭal pāyirat tiyalpē.

e-ṉ, pāyiram potuc ciṟappeṉa (cū. 2) mēl niṟutta muṟiyāṉē "malartali yulakiṉ" eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattavāy ellā nūṟkaṇṇum varum ciṟappuppāyiraṅkaḷiṉatu potuvilakkaṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

itaṉiyum potuppāyirattuṭ kūṟiṉār, potu aintaṉuḷ nūṉmukattiṉatu ilakkaṇamātaliṉeṉka.

(i-ḷ.) ivveṭṭup poruḷiyum viḷaṅka uṇarttuvatu ciṟappuppāyirattiṉatu ilakkaṇamām e-ṟu.

ākkiyōṉ peyarē yeṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉavaṟṟuṭ ciṟappeṉpatu tōṉṟiṉamiyiṟ pāyirameṉa vāḷā kūṟiṉāreṉka.

NA_ca_48

kālaṅ kaḷaṉē kāraṇa meṉṟim
mūvaki yēṟṟi moḻinaru muḷarē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) avveṭṭiṉōṭu immūṉṟiṉiyuṅ kūṭṭip patiṉoṉṟākak kūṟuvārumuḷar e-ṟu.

((nūṟpeyarvaki))

NA_ca_49

mutaṉūl karutta ṉaḷavu mikuti
poruḷcey vittōṉ ṟaṉmimuta ṉimittiṉum
iṭukuṟi yāṉunūṟ keytum peyarē.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉoṉṟaṉuḷ nūṟpeyarkkum yāppiṟkum ciṟappuvitiyuṇarttavēṇṭutaliṉ avviraṇṭaṉuḷ nūṟpeyarkkuc ciṟappuviti uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mutaṉūṉmutal taṉmiyīṟākak kūṟiya ēḻum piṟavumākiya kāraṇaṅkaḷālum iṭukuṟiyāṉum nūṟkup peyarvarum e-ṟu.

varalāṟu:- mutaṉūlāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa: āriyappaṭalam, pāratamutalāyiṉa; karuttaṉāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa: akattiyam, tolkāppiya mutalāyiṉa; aḷaviṉāṟ peṟṟaṉa: paṉṉirupaṭalam, nālaṭināṉūṟu mutalāyiṉa; mikutiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa: kaḷaviyal mutalāyiṉa; poruḷāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa: akapporuḷ mutalāyiṉa; ceyvittōṉāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa: cātavākaṉam, iḷantiriya mutalāyiṉa; taṉmiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa cintāmaṇi, naṉṉūl mutalāyiṉa; iṭukuṟiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa: nikaṇṭu, kalikkōṭṭuttaṇṭu mutalāyiṉa; piṟavumaṉṉa.

((nūlyāppu))

NA_ca_50

tokuttal viritta ṟokiviri moḻipeyarp
peṉattaku nūlyāp pīriraṇ ṭeṉpa.

e-ṉ, yāppiṉukkuc ciṟappuviti uṇarttuta ṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūlyāppu innāṉku kūṟṟatā meṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

veṇpāmutaliya yāppiṉōṭu ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟṟumi tōṉṟa nūlyāppeṉṟār.

((ciṟappuppāyirañ ceytaṟkuriyōr))

NA_ca_51

taṉṉā ciriyaṉ ṟaṉṉoṭu kaṟṟōṉ
taṉmā ṇākkaṉ takumuri kāraṉeṉ
ṟiṉṉōr pāyira miyamputal kaṭaṉē.

e-ṉ, ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyuṭiya ciṟappuppāyiram kūṟutaṟ kuriyār ivareṉa uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) oruvaṉ kūṟiya nūṟku innālvaruḷoruvar ciṟappuppāyiram kūṟutal muṟimi e-ṟu.

ciṟappuppāyiram piṟarāṟ ceyyappaṭutaṟkuk kāraṇam

NA_ca_52

tōṉṟā tōṟṟit tuṟipala muṭippiṉum
tāṉṟaṟ pukaḻta ṟakuti yaṉṟē.

e-ṉ, ciṟappuppāyiram piṟar kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) tōṉṟāta nuṭpaṅkaḷellām tōṟṟip pala tuṟippaṭṭu virintanūlic ceytumuṭittāṉāyiṉum taṉṉit tāṉ pukaḻtal takutiyaṉṟāmātaliṉ, nūl ceytāṉatu pukaḻākiya ciṟappuppāyirattip piṟar kūṟavēṇṭum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_53

maṉṉuṭi maṉṟat tōlit tūkkiṉum
taṉṉuṭi yāṟṟa luṇarā riṭiyiṉum
maṉṉiya vaviyiṭi velluṟu poḻutiṉum
taṉṉi maṟutali paḻitta kāliyum
taṉṉip pukaḻtalun takumpula vōrkkē.

e-ṉ, taṟpukaḻcciyum orōvaḻik kuṟṟamaṉṟeṉpatu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviṭaṅkaḷāyiṉ, taṉṉip pukaḻtalum takutiyām pulavōrkku e-ṟu.

pukaḻtalumeṉṟa ummiyāṉ ivviṭaṅkaḷilum taṉṉip pukaḻāmiyē takutiyeṉpatu peṟṟām.

((pāyiram nūlukku iṉṟiyamiyātateṉpatu))

NA_ca_54

āyira mukattā ṉakaṉṟa tāyiṉum
pāyira millatu paṉuva laṉṟē.

e-ṉ, iruvakip pāyiraṅkaḷum nūṟku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa veṉpatu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) āyiram uṟuppukkaḷāṉ virinta nūlāyiṉum pāyiramillātatu nūlaṉṟu e-ṟu.

((veṇpā))

NA_ca_55

māṭakkuc cittiramu mānakarkkuk kōpuramum
āṭamittō ṇallārk kaṇiyumpōl nāṭimuṉ
aituriyā niṉṟa vaṇinturiyi yennūṟkum
peyturiyā vittār peritu.

e-ṉ, mēṟpāyiramillatu paṉuvalaṉṟeṉṟār, aḵtu aṉṟātaṟkuk kāraṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) aṟivuṭiyōr māṭamutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum cittira mutaliya mūṉṟumpōlak karuti aḻakiyatākiya poruḷi uṇarttāniṉṟa iruvakippāyiraṅkaḷiyu murittu evvakippaṭṭa periya nūlkaṭkum muṉṉarp peytuvittār perumpālum; ātaliṟ pāyiramillatu paṉuvalaṉṟām e-ṟu.

((mutalāvatu eḻuttatikāram))

((mutalāvatu eḻuttiyal))

((kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkamum atikāramum))

NA_ca_56

pūmali yacōkiṉ puṉiniḻa lamarnta
nāṉmukaṟ ṟoḻutunaṉ kiyampuva ṉeḻuttē.

e-ṉ, kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkamum atikāramum uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pūkkaḷ malinta acōkamarattiṉatu alaṅkarikkum niḻaliṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷiyirunta nāṉku tirumukaṅkaḷiyuṭiya kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅki naṉṟākac colluvaṉ eḻuttilakkaṇatti e-ṟu.

ellā nūlum maṅkalamoḻi mutalvakuttuk kūṟavēṇṭutaliṟ pūmaliyeṉṟum, ellāc camayattōrālum vaṇaṅkappaṭum paṭippumutaliya aintoḻiṟkumuriya ellākkaṭavuḷākiyum niṉṟāṉoruvaṉē yeṉpār arukaṉi nāṉmakaṉeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. ivvāṟu vaḷḷuvanāyaṉārum.
"malarmiki yēkiṉāṉ māṇaṭi cērntār
nilamici nīṭuvāḻ vār" (kuṟaḷ, 3)

eṉa variyātu kūṟutal kāṇka. eḻutteṉpatu ākupeyar; ēkāram īṟṟaci. ellāmvalla kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkalāṉ iṉitu muṭiyumeṉpatu karuti naṉkiyampuvaṉeṉṟu pukuntamiyiṉ itu nutalippukutaleṉṉum utti.

((eḻuttilakkaṇa vaki))

NA_ca_57

eṇpeyar muṟipiṟap puruva māttiri
mutalī ṟiṭinili pōli yeṉṟā
patampuṇar peṉappaṉ ṉirupāṟ ṟatuvē.

e-ṉ, avveḻuttilakkaṇam ittuṇitteṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ippaṉṉirupakutiyiṉiyu muṭittu avveḻuttilakkaṇam e-ṟu.

eṉṟāveṉpatu eṇṇiṭiccol. iccūttiram tokuttuc cuṭṭaleṉṉum utti; mēlvaruvaṉavellām vakuttuk kāṭṭal.

1. eḻuttiṉ eṇ

NA_ca_58

moḻimutaṟ kāraṇa māmaṇut tiraḷoli
eḻuttatu mutalcār peṉaviru vakittē.

e-ṉ, "paṉṉiru pāṟṟatuvē" (cū. 57) eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, eḻuttiṉatu akattilakkaṇamākiya pattiyum ōriyalākavum, ataṉ puṟattilakkaṇamākiya patam puṇarpeṉṉumiraṇṭaṉuḷ avveḻuttāṉākum patatti ōriyalākavum, appatam puṇarum puṇarppi mūṉṟiyalākavum ōttumuṟivippeṉṉum uttiyāṉvikkappukuntu mutaṟkaṇ vitta eḻuttiyalil eḻuttiṉatu eṇṇiṉi niṟutta muṟiyāṉ uṇarttuvāṉṟoṭaṅki eḻuttu iṉṉateṉpatūum ataṉ vakiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇamāy aṇuttiraḷiṉ kāriyamāy varum oliyāvatu; eḻuttu; atu mutaleḻutteṉṟum cārpeḻutteṉṟum iruvakiyiṉi uṭittu e-ṟu.

eṉavē moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇam eḻuttāṉāṟpōla eḻuttukku mutaṟkāraṇam aṇuttiraḷeṉpatu peṟṟām. āmeṉṉum peyareccam oliyeṉṉum peyaroṭu muṭintatu. muṟku vīḷi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku mutaṟkāraṇamāy aṇuttiraḷiṉ kāriyamāy varumoli eḻuttākāmiyiṉ, moḻi mutaṟkāraṇamām oliyeṉṟār. citalatu nīrvāyc ciṟu tukaḷāṟ perumpuṟṟuruvaminta peṟṟiya teṉṉa aimpulap pēruruvintum aintaṇuvāl impariṟ camiva tiyāvaru maṟitaliṉ, anāti kāraṇamākiya māyiyiṉi īṇṭak kūṟātu ātikāraṇamākiya cevippulaṉām aṇuttiraḷi eḻuttiṟku mutaṟkāraṇameṉṟār. ivvāciriyarkku māyi uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu; aṇuttiraḷoṉṟumē tuṇiveṉiṉ, piṟitoṭupaṭāṉ taṉmataṅ koḷaleṉṉum matampaṭak kūṟiṉāreṉṟuṇarka īṇṭu aṇuveṉṟatu oliyiṉatu nuṭpatti.

((mutaleḻuttu))

NA_ca_59

uyiru muṭampumā muppatu mutalē.

e-ṉ, mutaleḻuttiṉ viri uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) uyirum uṭampumākum muppateḻuttum mutaleḻuttām e-ṟu.

((cārpeḻuttu))

NA_ca_60

uyirmey yāyta muyiraḷa poṟṟaḷa
paḵkiya iu aiau maḵkāṉ
taṉinili pattuñ cārpeḻut tākum.

e-ṉ, cārpeḻuttiṉ viri uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ippattum cārpeḻuttām e-ṟu.

aḵkutal - curuṅkutal. taṉinili - āytam. uyirkaḷōṭum meykaḷōṭuṅ kūṭiyum kūṭātum alipōlat taṉi niṟṟaliṉ taṉiniliyeṉappaṭum. uyirmey, uyirummeyyum kūṭippiṟattalāṉum, āytam uyirpōla,
"aṟṟā laḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu
peṟṟā ṉeṭituykku māṟu" (kuṟaḷ. 943)

eṉa alaku peṟṟum, meypōla,

"tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka vaḵtilār
tōṉṟaliṟ ṟōṉṟāmi naṉṟu" (kuṟaḷ, 236)

eṉa alaku peṟātum orupuṭi yottu avaṟṟiṉ iṭiyē cārntuvarutalāṉum, ēṉiya tattam mutaleḻuttiṉ tiripu vikārattāṟ piṟattalāṉum cārpeḻuttāyiṉa veṉak koḷka.

((cārpeḻuttiṉ viri))

NA_ca_61

uyirmey yiraṭṭunūṟ ṟeṭṭuya rāytam
eṭṭuyi raḷapeḻu mūṉṟoṟ ṟaḷapeṭi
āṟē ḻaḵku mimmup pāṉēḻ
ukara māṟā ṟikāṉ mūṉṟē
aukā ṉoṉṟē maḵkāṉ mūṉṟē
āyta miraṇṭoṭu cārpeḻut tuṟuviri
oṉṟoḻi munnūṟ ṟeḻupā ṉeṉpa.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) uyirmey irunūṟṟuppatiṉāṟu; kuṟukāta āytam eṭṭu; uyiraḷapeṭi irupattoṉṟu; oṟṟaḷapeṭi nāṟpattiraṇṭu; kuṟṟiyalikaram muppattēḻu; kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟu; aikārakkuṟukkam mūṉṟu; aukārak kuṟukkam oṉṟu; makarakkuṟukkam mūṉṟu; āytakkuṟukkam iraṇṭuṭaṉē cārpeḻuttiṉatu mikunta viri munnūṟṟaṟupattoṉpatāmeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

ivi ittuṇiyavātal piṟappatikārattiṉuṭ kāṇka. ivvāṟu uyirmey oḻintaṉavaṟṟiyum virittal toṉṉeṟiyeṉpār, eṉpaveṉṟār.

((peyarkaḷiṉ potuvilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_62

iṭukuṟi kāraṇap peyarpotuc ciṟappiṉa.

e-ṉ, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē eḻuttiṉ peyarāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉṟoṭaṅkip peyarkkellām potuvilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) iṭukuṟippeyarum kāraṇappeyarumākiya iraṇṭum pala poruṭkup potuppeyarākiyum oru poruṭkuc ciṟappup peyarākiyum varuvaṉavām e-ṟu.

oru poruḷik kuṟittaṟkuk kaṭavuḷāṉum aṟivuṭiyōrāṉum iṭṭakuṟiyākiya peyar iṭukuṟippeyar; kāraṇattāṉ varumpeyar kāraṇappeyar.

(u-m.) marameṉpatu iṭukuṟippotuppeyar. paṉiyeṉpatu iṭukuṟic ciṟappuppeyar. aṇiyeṉpatu kāraṇappotuppeyar. muṭiyeṉpatu kāraṇac ciṟappuppeyar.

iṉi iraṭṭuṟamoḻitaleṉṉumuttiyāṉ iccūttirattiṟku iṭukuṟiyeṉṟum kāraṇameṉṟum collappaṭum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyuṭiya peyarkaḷ iṭukuṟikāraṇameṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum potuvāyum iṭukuṟikkē ciṟappāyum kāraṇattiṟkē ciṟappāyum varuvaṉavāmeṉṟum poruḷurittuk koḷka.

(u-m.) marameṉpatu iṭukuṟippeyar. paramaṉeṉṟatu kāraṇappeyar. mukkaṇaṉ, antaṇaṉ, maṟavaṉ, muḷḷi, kaṟaṅku, moḻi, colleṉpaṉa kāraṇa iṭukuṟippeyar. ivi kāraṇaviṭukuṟiyāyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ivaṟṟuḷ mukkaṇaṉeṉṉum peyar āṉimukakkaṭavuḷ mutaliyōrkkum mūṉṟu kaṇṇuḷavātaliṟ kāraṇaṅkarutiyavaḻi mūṉṟu kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiyōr palarkkum cēṟalāṉum kāraṇaṅkarutāvaḻi iṭukuṟi māttiriyēyāyp paramaṉukkē cēṟalāṉum kāraṇa viṭukuṟiyāyiṟṟu. ēṉip peyarkaṭkum ivvāṟē kāṇka. ivvāṟē vaṭanūlār iṭukuṟiyi rūṭiyeṉṟum kāraṇatti yōkameṉṟum kāraṇa viṭukuṟiyi yōkarūṭi yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.

ivvilakkaṇattāṉ eḻuttiṉatu peyarum piṟapeyarum varumāṟu kāṇka.

2. eḻuttiṉ peyar

NA_ca_63

ammuta līrā ṟāvi kammutal
meymmū vāṟeṉa viḷampiṉar pulavar.

e-ṉ, orucāreḻuttiṉatu peyarāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) akaramutal aukāravīṟākak kiṭanta paṉṉiraṇṭiṉiyum āviyeṉṟum kakaramutalākak kiṭanta patiṉeṭṭiṉiyum meyyeṉṟum nūlkaḷāṟ coṉṉār aṟivuṭiyōr e-ṟu.

kaṭavuḷāl āvimeyyeṉṟu amitta peyarkkāraṇam uyirkaḷāṉ muṟṟum uṇartaṟku arumiyum kaṭavuṇūl uṇarntōrvaḻic cellum tamatu perumiyum tōṉṟa viḷampiṉar pulavareṉṟār. āvimeyyumpōṟaliṉ ivviruvakiyeḻuttiṟkum āvi meyyeṉpaṉa uvamavākupeyarāyk kāraṇappotuppeyarāyiṉa. ēṉiyavum ivvāṟē kāṇka.

((kuṟil))

NA_ca_64

avaṟṟuḷ
ai ue okkuṟi lintē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) āvi mey eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ ivvintum kuṟṟeḻuttām e-ṟu.

((neṭil))

NA_ca_65

āī ūē aiō au neṭil.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvēḻum neṭṭeḻuttām e-ṟu.

((cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ))

NA_ca_66

ai ummutaṟ ṟaṉivariṟ cuṭṭē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) immūṉṟeḻuttum moḻikkuppuṟattum akattum mutaṟkaṇ taṉittuc cuṭṭup poruḷuṇarttavariṟ cuṭṭeḻuttām e-ṟu.

mutaleṉap potuppaṭakkūṟiṉamiyiṟ puṟattum akattumeṉpatu peṟṟām.

(u-m.) akkoṟṟaṉ ikkoṟṟaṉ ukkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum, avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ eṉavum varum.

avaṉeṉpataṉkaṇ akaram aṟameṉpataṉkaṇ akaram pōlap piṉ eḻuttukkaḷōṭu toṭarntuniṉṟu oruporuḷiyuṇarttātu maliyaṉeṉpataṉkaṭ pakutipōla vēṟu niṉṟu cuṭṭupporuḷuṇarttaliṉ akattuvarum itaṉiyum taṉivariṉeṉṟār. ivvuri viṉāviṟkuṅ koḷka.

((viṉāveḻuttukkaḷ))

NA_ca_67

eyā mutalum āō īṟṟum
ēiru vaḻiyum viṉavā kummē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) puṟattum akattum moḻimutaṟkaṇ ekaramum yāvum, īṟṟiṉkaṇ ākāramum ōkāramum, ivviraṇṭiṭattiṉum ēkāramum taṉittu viṉāpporuḷuṇarttavariṉ viṉāveḻuttām e-ṟu.

mēl taṉivariṉeṉṟataṉi īṇṭum kūṭṭuka. ivi puṟattum akattum varutal ēṟṟapeṟṟi koḷka.

oṉṟiṉamuṭittal taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ yāviṉāviyum uṭaṉkūṟiṉār.

(u-m.) ekkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum evaṉ, yāvaṉ eṉavum, mutaṟkaṇ vantaṉa. koṟṟaṉā, koṟṟaṉō veṉa īṟṟiṉkaṇ vantaṉa. ēvaṉ, koṟṟaṉē eṉa īriṭattum vantaṉa. ēṉip peyarviṉikaḷōṭum ēṟṟa peṟṟi oṭṭikkoḷka.

((valliṉam))

NA_ca_68

valliṉaṅ kacaṭata paṟaveṉa vāṟē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟum valliṉamām e-ṟu.

((melliṉam))

NA_ca_69

melliṉam ṅañaṇana maṉaveṉa vāṟē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟum melliṉamām e-ṟu.

((iṭiyiṉam))

NA_ca_70

iṭiyiṉam yaralava ḻaḷaveṉa vāṟē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟum iṭiyiṉamām e-ṟu.

((iṉaveḻuttu))

NA_ca_71

aiau iuc ceṟiya mutaleḻut
tivviraṇ ṭōriṉa māyvaraṉ muṟiyē.

e-ṉ, iṉamaṭittal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) iṉamillāta aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ īkāra ūkāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya ikara ukaraṅkaḷit tamakkum iṉamākap porunta mutaleḻuttukkaḷ ivviraṇṭu ōriṉamāy varutal muṟiyātalāl avi iṉaveḻutteṉṟum peyar peṟum e-ṟu.

((iṉameṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇam))

NA_ca_72

tāṉa muyaṟci yaḷavu poruḷvaṭi
vāṉavoṉ ṟātiyōr puṭiyop piṉamē.

e-ṉ, mēl iṉameṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) tāṉamutaliyavāya ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟumutalāka orupuṭi yottalāl iṉamām e-ṟu.

tāṉam - ura mutaliyaṉa: muyaṟci - itaḻmuyaṟci mutaliyaṉa: aḷavu - māttiri: poruḷ - pālaṉ viruttaṉāṉāṟpōlak kuṟiliṉatu vikāramē neṭilātaliṉ iraṇṭaṟkum poruḷoṉṟeṉṟu mutaṉūlāl niyamikkappaṭṭa poruḷ: vaṭivu - oli vaṭivu, varivaṭivu. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟum palavumottu iṉamāy varutal kaṇṭukoḷka.

cārpeḻuttiṟkup peyar kūṟātoḻintār, āytam taṉi niliyātalāṉum ēṉic cārpeḻuttiṟkuc cuṭṭuviṉāveṉṟu vitantōtiṉaviyoḻinta mutaleḻuttiṉ peyarē peyarāyaṭaṅkutalāṉu meṉka.

iccūttiram ētuviṉmuṭitta leṉṉum utti.

3. eḻuttiṉ muṟi

NA_ca_73

ciṟappiṉu miṉattiṉuñ ceṟintīṇ ṭammutal
naṭatta ṟāṉē muṟiyā kummē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē muṟiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ciṟappiṉāṉum iṉattiṉāṉum porunti ivvulakattu akaramutalāka vaḻaṅkutaṟāṉē eḻuttiṉatu muṟiyām e-ṟu.

kuṟiliṉatu vikāramē neṭilātalāṟ kuṟil muṉṉiṟṟaliṟ ciṟappiṉeṉṟum, neṭil iṉamāyp piṉṉiṟṟaliṉ iṉattiṉeṉṟum, nātamāttiriyāy ellā eḻuttiṟkum kāraṇamāy muṉṉiṟṟaliṉ ammutaleṉṟum, ellāveḻuttiṟkum vitta muṟikkāraṇam uyirkaḷāṉ muṟṟumuṇartalākāmiyiṉ naṭattaṟāṉēyeṉṟum kūṟiṉār.

4. eḻuttiṉ piṟappu

NA_ca_74

niṟiyuyir muyaṟciyi ṉuḷvaḷi turappa
eḻumaṇut tiraḷuraṅ kaṇṭa mucci
mūkkuṟ ṟitaḻnāp pallaṇat toḻiliṉ
ve/vē ṟeḻuttoli yāyvaral piṟappē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē piṟappāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉ toṭaṅki ataṉ potuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) oliyeḻuttākiya kāriyattiṟku vēṇṭum kāraṇaṅkaḷil oruvāṟṟāṉum kuṟiviṉṟi niṟinta uyiriṉatu muyaṟciyāl uṇṇiṉṟa vaḷiyeḻuppa eḻum cevippulaṉām aṇukkaḷ uramutaliya nāṉkiṭattiyum porunti itaḻ mutaliya nāṉkiṉ toḻilkaḷāl vevvēṟu eḻuttākiya olikaḷāyt tōṉṟutal avaṟṟiṉ piṟappām e-ṟu.

((mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṭappiṟappu))

NA_ca_75

avvaḻi,
āvi yiṭimi yiṭamiṭa ṟākum
mēvu meṉmimūk kurampeṟum vaṉmi.

e-ṉ, mutaleḻuttukkaṭku iṭamvakuttal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiyaneṟiyāṟ piṟakkuṅkāl, āvi iṭiyiṉameṉṉum iruvaki yeḻuttiṉatu iṭam miṭaṟākum; melliṉam mūkki iṭamākap poruntum; valliṉam neñci iṭamākap peṟum e-ṟu.

mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ muyaṟcip piṟappu

NA_ca_76

avaṟṟuḷ,
muyaṟciyuḷ aā vaṅkāp puṭiya.

e-ṉ, mutaleḻuttuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muyaṟci vakiyāṟ piṟappuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēl iṭamvakukkappaṭṭa mutaleḻuttukkaḷuḷ, akara ākāraṅkaḷ nālvaki muyaṟciyuḷ aṇṇattiṉ toḻilākiya aṅkāttali yuṭiyavāyp piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_77

iī eē aiaṅ kāppō
ṭaṇpaṉ mutaṉā viḷimpuṟa varumē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvinteḻuttum aṅkāppuṭaṉē aṇpalli aṭinā viḷimpu poruntap piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_78

uū oō auvitaḻ kuvivē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvinteḻuttum piṟattaṟku ētuvāya muyaṟci itaḻkuvivām e-ṟu.

NA_ca_79

kaṅavuñ cañavum ṭaṇavu mutaliṭi
nuṉinā vaṇṇa muṟamuṟi varumē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) kavvum ṅavvum mutaṉā mutalaṇṇattiyum, cavvum ñavvum iṭinā iṭiyaṇṇattiyum, ṭavvum ṇavvum nuṉinā nuṉiyaṇṇattiyumu/ porunta immuṟiyē piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_80

aṇpal laṭinā muṭiyuṟat tanavarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṇpallaṭiyi nānuṉi poruntat tavvum navvum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_81

mīkī ḻitaḻuṟap pammap piṟakkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mēlitaḻum kīḻitaḻum poruntap pavvum mavvum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_82

aṭinā vaṭiyaṇa muṟayat tōṉṟum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṭinā aṭiyaṇṇattip porunta yakaram piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_83

aṇṇa nuṉinā varuṭa raḻavarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṇṇatti nuṉināt taṭava ravvum ḻavvum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_84

aṇpaṉ mutalu maṇṇamu muṟiyiṉ
nāviḷimpu vīṅki yoṟṟavum varuṭavum
lakāra ḷakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṇpaṉmutali nāviḷimpu vīṅki oṟṟa lakāramākiyum, aṇṇatti nāviḷimpu vīṅki varuṭa ḷakāramākiyum ivviraṇṭeḻuttum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_85

mēṟpal litaḻuṟa mēviṭum vavvē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟpal kīḻitaḻip porunta vakāram piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_86

aṇṇa nuṉinā naṉiyuṟiṟ ṟaṉavarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṇṇatti nuṉinā mikapporuntil ṟavvum ṉavvum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

cārpeḻuttiṉ iṭamum muyaṟciyum

NA_ca_87

āytak kiṭantali yaṅkā muyaṟci
cārpeḻut tēṉavun tammuta laṉiya.

e-ṉ, cārpeḻuttukkaṭku iṭamuyaṟci vakiyāṟ piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) āytam piṟattaṟku iṭam tali; muyaṟci aṅkāttalām; oḻinta cārpeḻuttukkaḷum iṭamuyaṟcikaḷ tam mutaleḻuttukkaḷōṭu oppaṉavāyp piṟakkum e-ṟu.

cārpiṉ cārpākiya āytakkuṟukkamum ataṉ mutal pōlap piṟakkumeṉpār etiratutaḻīi eṉavumeṉṟār.

((piṟappiṉ puṟaṉaṭi))

NA_ca_88

eṭuttal paṭutta ṉalita luḻappiṟ
ṟiripun tattamiṟ ciṟituḷa vākum.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya piṟappiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) palaveḻuttiṟkum piṟappu oṉṟākac collappaṭṭaṉavēṉum eṭuttal paṭuttal nalitaleṉṉum eḻuttiṟkuriya olimuyaṟciyāṉ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu piṟappu vēṟupāṭukaḷum avvavaṟṟiṉkaṭ ciṟitu uḷavām e-ṟu.

((uyirmey))

NA_ca_89

puḷḷivaṭ ṭavvoṭu muṉṉuru vākiyum
ēṉi yuyirō ṭuruvu tirintum
uyiraḷa vāyataṉ vaṭivoḻit tiruvayiṟ
peyaroṭu moṟṟumuṉ ṉāyvaru muyirmey.

e-ṉ, mēṟ piṟappuṇarttiya cārpeḻuttiṟkuc cārntu varumāṟum oru piṟappātaliṟ piṟappatikārattuḷ aṭakki uṇarttuvāṉṟoṭaṅki avaṟṟuḷ uyirmey varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mey puḷḷiyi viṭṭu akarattōṭu kūṭiyavaḻi viṭṭa uruvē uruvākiyum oḻinta uyirkaḷōṭu kūṭiya vaḻi uruvuvēṟu paṭṭum taṉmāttiri tōṉṟātu uyirmāttiriyē māttiriyāy ataṉ varivaṭiviṉatu vikāravaṭivē vaṭivāy uyir vaṭivi oḻittu meyyuyireṉṉum peyarākiya iraṇṭiṭattum piṟanta uyirmeyyeṉṉum peyaruṭaṉē oṟṟoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉumāki varum uyirmeyyeḻuttu e-ṟu.

oḻitteṉṟum oṟṟumuṉṉāyeṉṟum vanta ciṉiviṉikaḷ iruvayiṟ peyaroṭum varumeṉṉum mutalviṉiyotu muṭintaṉa.

pūti mutaliyavaṟṟiṟṟīṭṭiya varivaṭivum olivaṭivupōlap payaṉṟarum oṟṟumi kuṟittu īṇṭu avvirutiṟaṉum viravik kūṟiṉār.

ivvilakkaṇattāṉ ovvoru meyyiṟ paṉṉīruyiruṅ kūṭap paṉṉirupatiṉeṭ ṭirunūṟṟorupattāṟāy varumāṟu vaḻaṅkum varivaṭivuḷḷum olivaṭivuḷḷum kāṇka.

((muṟṟāytam))

NA_ca_90

kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micittē.

e-ṉ, muṟṟāytam varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) puḷḷivaṭiviṉatāy āytam kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāy uyiroṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttāṟaṉmēlatāy varum e-ṟu.

uyirvarukkattu iṟutikkaṭṭanta āytavaṭivip piṟkālattu vēṟupaṭa varintu vaḻaṅkuvarātaliṉ, tolli vaṭivutōṉṟa āytappuḷḷiyeṉṟār.

(u-m.) eḵku, kaḵcu, irupaḵtu, aḵkaṭiya, aḵkāṉ, vaḵkāṉ eṉavum taṉikkuṟileṉṟu varintōtāmiyiṉ
"vilaḵḵki vīṅkiru ḷōṭṭumē mātar
ilaḵḵku mutti ṉiṉam"

eṉavum varum. piṟavumaṉṉa.

iccūttiram mēṟkōḷātalāl tāṉeṭuttu moḻitaleṉṉu mutti. valliṉavakiyāṉ iyalpākavanta āytam āṟaṉōṭu puṇarccivikārattāṉ varumāytamum ceyyuḷ vikārattāṉ varumāytamum kūṭi eṭṭāy varumāṟu kāṇka.

((uyiraḷapeṭi))

NA_ca_91

icikeṭiṉ moḻimuta liṭikaṭi nilineṭil
aḷapeḻu mavaṟṟavaṟ ṟiṉakkuṟil kuṟiyē.

e-ṉ, uyiraḷapeṭi varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ceyyuṭkaṇ ici kuṉṟiṉ moḻimutaliṉum iṭiyiṉum kaṭiyiṉum niṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttēḻum avvici niṟikkat tam māttiriyiṉ mikkolikkum: avvāṟu aḷapeṭuttami aṟitaṟku avaṟṟiṉpiṉ avaṟṟiṟku iṉamākiya kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ varivaṭiviṉkaṇ aṟikuṟiyāy varum. e-ṟu. (u-m.)
"āa aḷiya valavaṉṟaṉ pārppiṉō
ṭīi ririyuṅkoṇ ṭīraḷip paḷḷiyuṭ
ṭūun tiriyalippat tuñcā tiṟivaṉṟōḷ
mēē valippaṭṭanampō ṉaṟunutāl
ōovu ḻakkun tuyar"

eṉavum,
"uṟāark kuṟunō yurippāy kaṭalic
ceṟāaay vāḻiyaveṉ ṉeñcu" (kuṟaḷ. 1200)

eṉavum,
"aṉiccappūk kālkaḷiyāḷ peytā ṇucuppiṟku
nalla paṭāa paṟi" (kuṟaḷ. 1115)

eṉavum varum. `ceṟāaay vāḻiya' eṉpuḻi nāṉku māttiri yāyum ēṉiya mūṉṟumāttiriyāyum alakupeṟṟuc ceyyuḷici niṟittu mūviṭattum vantavāṟu kāṇka. piṟavumaṉṉa.

iṉi ici#eṭiṉeṉṟu potuppaṭak kūṟiṉamiyāṉ,
"keṭuppatūuṅ keṭṭārkkuc cārvāymaṟ ṟāṅkē
eṭuppatūu mellā maḻi" (kuṟaḷ. 15)

eṉak kuṟilneṭilāy aḷapeḻuntu alakupeṟṟum peṟātum iṉṉici niṟippa varuvaṉavum,
"uraṉacii yuḷḷan tuṇiyākac ceṉṟār
varaṉacii yiṉṉu muḷēṉ" (kuṟaḷ, 1263)

eṉa aḷapeḻuntu alakupeṟṟum peṟātum collici niṟippa varuvaṉavum.
"vīḻappaṭuvār keḻīiyilar tāḻvīḻvār
vīḻap paṭāa reṉiṉ" (kuṟaḷ. 1194)

eṉpataṉuṭ keḻīiyilareṉa aḷapeḻuntu alaku peṟātu collici niṟippa varuvaṉavum koḷka.

eḻuttup palavāyiṉa olivēṟṟumiyāṉaṉṟē: aṅṅaṉamāka, neṭilatu vikāramāy ōroliyāyp piṟakkum aḷapeṭi iraṇṭeḻuttuk kūṭi mūṉṟumāttiriyāyiṟṟeṉak koḷḷiṉ, iraṇṭeḻuttoli aṅṅaṉam iṉmiyāṉum aḷapeṭiyeṉṉum peyar ēlāmiyāṉum avvāṟu koḷḷātu aṟikuṟiyēyeṉṟu kōṭaṟkuk kuṟiyēyeṉṟār. ikkaruttēpaṟṟi āciriyar tolkāppiyarum, "(kuṉṟici moḻivayi ṉiṉṟici niṟikku, neṭṭeḻuttu" eṉṟum `avaṟṟiṉpiṉ aṟikuṟiyāy varum ottakuṟṟeḻuttu' eṉṟum kūṟiṉāraṉṟē? kuṟiyē yeṉṟatu, ivviraṇṭu mēṟkōḷil oṉṟu tuṇitalāṉ orutali tuṇitaleṉṉu muttiyum ammatamumpaṭa vantateṉka.

itaṉuḷ, iṭavakiyāṉ uyiraḷapeṭi eḻumūṉṟāy varumāṟu kāṇka.

((oṟṟaḷapeṭi))

NA_ca_92

ṅañaṇa namaṉa vayalaḷa vāytam
aḷapāṅ kuṟiliṇi kuṟiṟkī yiṭikaṭi
mikalē yavaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyām vēṟē.

e-ṉ, oṟṟaḷapeṭi varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ceyyuṭkaṇ icikuṉṟiṉ, kuṟiliṇik kīḻum kuṟiṟkīḻumāy moḻiyiṭiyiṉum īṟṟiṉum niṉṟa ippat toṟṟum āytamum avvōciyi niṟikkat tam māttiriyiṉ mikkolikkum; ivvāṟu aḷapeṭuttaṉa aṟitaṟku avaṟṟiṉpiṉ avveḻuttukkaḷē varivaṭiviṉkaṇ vēṟu aṟikuṟiyāy varum e-ṟu.

"orumoḻi yoḻitaṉ ṉiṉaṅkoḷaṟ kurittē" (cū. 358) eṉpataṉāl mēl icikeṭiṉeṉṟataṉi ivvaḷapeṭikkum varuvittukkoḷka. māttiri niṟittaṟkaṉṟi aṟikuṟi māttiriyāy varumeṉṟu kōṭaṟku vēṟē yeṉṟār.

(u-m.)
"ilaṅṅku veṇpiṟicū ṭīcaṉaṭi yārkkuk
kalaṅṅku neñcamili kāṇ"

eṉavum,
"eṅṅ kiṟivaṉuḷa ṉeṉpāy maṉaṉēyāṉ
eṅṅ keṉattirivā riṉ"

eṉavum,
"kalaṅṅkoṇṭa kaviṉmārpar"
"maṭaṅṅ kalanta maṉaṉē kaḷattu
viṭaṅṅ kalantāṉi vēṇṭu"

eṉavum,

"aṅṅ kaṉinta varuḷiṭattārk kaṉpuceytu
naṅṅ kaḷaṅkaṟuppā nām"

eṉavum ṅakaram kuṟiliṇikkīḻum kuṟiṟkīḻum moḻikkiṭiyiṉum kaṭiyiṉum muṟiyē aḷapeḻuntatu.
"kaṇṇ karuviḷi kārmulli kūreyiṟu
poṉṉ poṟicuṇaṅku pōḻvā yilavampū
miṉṉ ṉuḻimaruṅku mētaku cāyalāḷ
eṉṉ piṟamakaḷā māṟu"

eṉa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum kuṟiṟkīḻ moḻiyīṟṟiṉ aḷapeḻuntaṉa.
"eḵḵ kilaṅkiya kiyarā yiṉṉuyir
veḵḵku vārkkilli vīṭu"

eṉa āytam kuṟiṟkīḻ moḻikkiṭiyiṉ aḷapeḻuntatu; piṟavumaṉṉa.

āytam oṟṟōṭum orupuṭiyottavarutaliṉ īṇṭut tanturittār. nakaram vitiyīṟāy varutaliṉ ataṉi vilakkātu, āytam oru moḻikkaṇṇum toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇum vititta mutaleḻuttukkaḷ irumaruṅku niṉṟeḻuppa iru ciṟakeḻuppa eḻumuṭalatupōl iṭiyeḻuntu olippataṉṟi oruvāṟṟāṉum īṟāyvarum taṉmiyataṉṟākaliṉ, aḵkaṭiya mutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ tirinta āytam aḵkāṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṭṭōṉṟiya āytam pōlat toṭarmoḻikkaṇ iṭi niliyāyataṉṟi vitiyīṟaṉṟeṉṟu ataṉuṇmi tuṇintu ataṉi iṟutikkaṇ vilakki, oṟṟaḷapeṭi nāṟpattiraṇṭeṉṟu mēlurittavāṟu iṅṅaṉam varutal kāṇka.

((kuṟṟiyalikaram))

NA_ca_93

yakaram varakkuṟa ḷuttiri yikaramum
aciccoṉ miyāvi ṉikaramuṅ kuṟiya.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟiyalikaram varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) yakaram varumoḻikku mutalākivara nilimoḻikku īṟāki niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukarantirinta ikaramum miyāveṉṉum aciccolliṉkaṇ ikaramum kuṟṟiyalikaraṅkaḷām e-ṟu.

eṉavē iṅṅaṉam varumāyiṉ ivveḻuttiṉṟoṭarpāṟ kuṟukumeṉpatu peṟṟām.

(u-m.) nākiyātu eḵkiyātu varakiyātu kokkiyātu kuraṅkiyātu alkiyātu eṉavum, kēṇmiyā ceṉmiyā eṉavum varum. piṟavu maṉṉa.

potuppaṭak kūṟiya kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟālum aciccol miyāviṉālum kuṟṟiyalikaram muppattēḻāy varumāṟu kāṇka.

((kuṟṟiyalukaram))

NA_ca_94

neṭilo ṭāyta muyirvali meliyiṭi
toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti vaṉmiyū rukaram
aḵkum piṟamēṟ ṟoṭaravum peṟumē.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) taṉineṭiluṭaṉē āytamum uyirum valloṟṟum melloṟṟum iṭiyoṟṟumākiya ellā veḻuttiṉuḷḷum yātāṉumoṉṟu toṭarappaṭṭa moḻiyiṟutikkaṇ valloṟṟukkaḷi ūrntuvanta ukaram taṉ māttiriyiṟ kuṟukum; atu taṉ māttiriyiṟ kuṟukutaṟkum moḻi niramputaṟkuṅ kāraṇam ivvōreḻuttuttoṭar taṉ māttiriyiṉ amiyātu piṟaveḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟum palavum mēlē toṭaravum peṟum e-ṟu.

neṭili oṭukkoṭuttup pirittār, taṉineṭilātaliṉ. uyireṉpatu kuṟṟuyir neṭṭuyir iraṇṭiṉiyum. toṭareṉṉum viṉittoki vaṉmiyūrukara meṉṉum ceyappaṭu poruḷoṭu muṭintatu. piṟaveṉṟatu pakāppatamēḻum pakupatamoṉpatu meṉṟu variyaṟuttavaṟṟiṉ iṅṅaṉam īṟṟayalum īṟumākak kūṟiya iraṇṭumoḻittu oḻinta aintum ēḻumāya eḻuttukkaḷi. toṭaravumeṉṟavummi iṟantatutaḻīi niṉṟatu. peṟumē yeṉṟatu, taṉineṭiloḻinta aintoṭarum vaṉmiyūrukaram kuṟukutaṟkum moḻi niramputaṟkum mēṟṟoṭartalum iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉ.

(u-m.) nāku, eḵku, varaku, palācu, kokku, kuraṅku, alku eṉa varum. piṟavu maṉṉa.

āciriyar tolkāppiyar.
"neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē" (moḻi. cū. 3.)

eṉat tokuttuk kūṟiyavāṟē kūṟātu ellā eḻuttiyum kūṟuvār. avaṟṟiyē āytamutal aintāka vakuttut taṉi neṭilōṭu āṟākak kūṟutal kuṟṟukaratti aṟuvakiyāka vaḻaṅkutaṟku ilakkaṇan tōṉṟaveṉak koḷka. itu muṉ moḻintu kōṭaleṉṉum utti. avvāciriyar nuntiyeṉṉu muṟippeyarkkaṇ ukarattik kuṟukkameṉṟār. aḵtu ivarkku uṭaṉpāṭaṉmiyiṟ kūṟātoḻintār. itu maṟuttaleṉṉum matam; ēṉiya uṭaṉpaṭal.

mayakkavitiyiṉmiyiṉ; iṭiyiṉantoṭarāta ṭakāra ṟakāraṅkaḷi ūrnta ukaramiṇṭiṉiyum vilakkātu āṉimukak kaṭavuḷi mummatatta ṉeṉṟāṟpōla ilakkaṇi yeṉṉum vitiyāṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟeṉṟu mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē vaṉmiyūrukaram āṟōṭu neṭil mutaliya āṟiyumuṟaḻa muppattāṟāy varumāṟu kāṇka.

((aikārakkuṟukkamum aukārakkuṟukkamum))

NA_ca_95

taṟcuṭ ṭaḷavoḻi yimmū vaḻiyum
niyu muvu mutalaṟ ṟākum.

e-ṉ, aikāra aukārakkuṟukkaṅkaḷ varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) aikāram taṉṉik karutit taṉpeyar kūṟumaḷaviṟ kuṟukātu, oḻinta moḻimutal kaṭi iṭiyeṉṉu mūṉṟiṭattum muṉṉum piṉṉum irumaruṅkumāya eḻuttiṉ toṭarpāl taṉ māttiriyiṟ kuṟukum. aukāramum taṉpeyar kūṟumaḷaviṟ kuṟukātu, oḻinta moḻi mutaṟkaṭ piṉvarumeḻuttiṉ toṭarpāl taṉ māttiriyiṟ kuṟukum e-ṟu.

cuṭṭaḷaveṉpatu viṉittoki. aḷaveṉṟār, eḻuttiṉ cāriyi toṭariṉum kuṟukumeṉpatu karuti. eṉavē ōreḻuttoru moḻiyāy nilimoḻi varumoḻiyāyt toṭarntu taṉittu ōrōviṭattuvariṉ, taṉmāttiri kuṟukāteṉpatūum peṟṟām.

(u-m.) aippaci mippuṟam eṉavum, valiyaṉ eṉavum, kuvaḷi eṉavum, muval eṉavum varum.

iṉi aḷapeṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu aḷapeṉpataṉi aḷapeṭiyākkit taṉṉiccollutaṟkaṇṇum aḷapeṭuttaṟkaṇṇum kuṟukāteṉap poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar. taṉṉiyalpāya iraṇṭumāttiriyiṉiṉṟum kuṟukutal illaṉavaṟṟi yoḻippār, vikārattāl mūṉṟumāttiriyum nāṉkumāttiriyumāy mikkolikkum aḷapeṭiyi oḻikkavēṇṭāmiyiṉ atu poruntāteṉka.

iṭavakiyāṉ aikārakkuṟukkaṅkaḷ mūṉṟum, aukārak kuṟukkam oṉṟum varumāṟu kāṇka.

((makarakkuṟukkam))

NA_ca_96

ṇaṉamuṉṉum vaḵkāṉ miciyumak kuṟukum.

e-ṉ, makarakkuṟukkam varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷil oṉṟu niṉṟutoṭarntiṭa ataṉmuṉvariṉum, vakaram vantu toṭarntiṭa ataṉmēl niṟpiṉum makaram taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukum e-ṟu.

(u-m.)
"pacuppōlvār muṟpaṭṭāṟ pāṟpaṭṭa cāṉṟōr
mucuppōla muḷkān tiruppar - pacuttāṉ
veruḷiṉu mellām veruḷumaḵ taṉṟi
maruḷiṉu mellā maruṇam" eṉavum,
"ticiyaṟi mīkāṉum pōṉm" (pari. 10 : 55) eṉavum,

"tarum vaḷavaṉ" eṉavum varum.

iṉi ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉvanta makaram ceyyumeṉṉum vāypāṭṭu muṟṟīṟṟateṉpatūum, avvīṟṟayalukaraṅkeṭa avvukaramūrnta ḷakara lakaraṅkaḷ īṟṟumakarattoṭu mayaṅkāmiyiṉ mayaṅkutaṟkuriya ṇakara ṉakaramāyt tirintaṉa īṇṭu niṉṟa ṇakara ṉakarameṉpatūum, ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟuṭaṉ īroṟṟāy mayaṅkiṉ makaraṅ kuṟukumeṉpatūum, ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ kuṟukutal ceyyuṭkaṇṇateṉpatūum, kāṇum kūṉumeṉa iyalpākaniṉṟa ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷmuṉ ivvāṟu vantu makaram kuṟukāteṉpatūum, piṟavum mēlvarum cūttiraṅkaḷellāvaṟṟiyumvēṇṭip paruntiṉvīḻvāy niṉṟa iccūttirattu uyttuṇarntu koḷka. iḵtu uyttuṇaravippeṉṉumutti.

((āytakkuṟukkam))

NA_ca_97

laḷavīṟ ṟiyipiṉā māyta maḵkum.

e-ṉ, āytakkuṟukkam varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) lakāra ḷakāravīṟṟup puṇarcciyiṉāl varum. "kuṟilvaḻi laḷattav vaṇiyi ṉāyta, mākavum peṟūum" (cū. 228) eṉṟa āytam irumaruṅkumāya eḻuttiṉ toṭarpāl taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu eṉa varum.

itu muṭiviṭaṅkūṟaleṉṉu mutti. ivvāytam kuṟukāteṉak kāṇṭalvirōtaṅ kūṟuvārumuḷar.

5. eḻuttiṉ uruvam

NA_ca_98

tolli vaṭiviṉa vellā veḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytu mekara mokaramey puḷḷi.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē uruvamāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ellāveḻuttum palavēṟuvakippaṭṭa varintu vaḻaṅkum paḻiya vaṭiviṉi yuṭiyavām; avvaṭiviṉavāy vaḻaṅkumiṭattut taṉittum uṭampūrntum varum ekaramum okaramum taṉimeykaḷum iyalpāya puḷḷiyip piṟkālattu oḻittuvarintu ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷōṭum uyirmeykaḷōṭum aiyappaṭa vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkiṉi yuṭiyavaṉṟit tuṇiyappaṭum tollivaṭiviṉatu uṟuppāya puḷḷiyip peṟum e-ṟu.

(va-ṟu.) e e; o o; ke ke; ko ko; ka ka eṉa varum.

6. eḻuttiṉ māttiri

NA_ca_99

mūṉṟuyi raḷapiraṇ ṭāneṭi loṉṟē
kuṟilō ṭiyuk kuṟukka moṟṟaḷa
pariyoṟ ṟiuk kuṟukka māytam
kālkuṟaṇ maḵkā ṉāyta māttiri.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē māttiriyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) uyiraḷapeṭikku māttiri mūṉṟām; neṭilukku māttiri iraṇṭām; kuṟilukkum aikārakkuṟukkattiṟkum aukārakkuṟukkattiṟkum oṟṟaḷapeṭikkum taṉittaṉi māttiri oṉṟām; oṟṟiṟkum kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟkum kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum āytattiṟkum taṉittaṉi māttiri ariyām; makarakkuṟukkattiṟkum āytak kuṟukkattiṟkum taṉittaṉi māttiri kālām e-ṟu.

uyirmeykku aḷavukūṟātoḻintār, mēl, uyiraḷavāyeṉṟurittaliṉ; iḵtu urittāmeṉṉu mutti. uyiraḷapeṭi nāṉkumāttiriyavātal āriṭattuḷḷum avi pōlpavaṟṟuḷḷum arukivantu ceyyuḷ vaḻuvamitiyāy muṭitaliṉ, ataṉiyoḻittu ellārkkum oppamuṭintu payiṉṟu varuvatu mūṉṟu māttiriyēyākalāṉ, mūṉṟuyiraḷapeṉṟār.

NA_ca_100

iyalpeḻu mānta riminoṭi māttiri.

e-ṉ, eḻuttoliyeḻucci, palavaṟṟiyum aḷantu kōṭaṟkukkūṟiya orumāttiriyeṉṉum kālacaṅkiyiṉi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) māntaruṭiya iyalpākaveḻum imippoḻutum noṭippoḻutum orumāttiri yeṉṉum variyaṟippoḻutām e-ṟu.

imiyeṉṟatu imittali; noṭiyeṉṟatu olittali. iviyiraṇṭum ākupeyarāyk kālattiyuṇarttiniṉṟaṉa. iyalpeḻumeṉṉum peyareccam imi noṭiyeṉṉum peyarkaḷōṭu muṭintatu. eḻuttoli mutaliyavaṟṟi iyalpu keṭuttu oruvaṉ vēṇṭiyavāṟē yeḻuppiṉum avvāṟeḻāniṟkum, imiyum noṭiyum iyalpukeṭutteḻuppa vēṇṭiṉum avvāṟē eḻātu iyalpākavē eḻāniṟkumātaliṉ iyalpeḻumeṉavum mēliccūttirattu eḻuttolikaḷi vēṇṭiyavāṟē eḻuppātu ivvaḷavāṉ aḷanteḻuppukveṉavum kūṟiṉār.

piṉṉatu niṟuttaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ iv aḷavukaruvi piṟkūṟappaṭṭatu.

NA_ca_101

āviyu moṟṟu maḷaviṟan ticittalum
mēvu miciviḷi paṇṭamāṟ ṟātiyiṉ.

e-ṉ, tavaḷippāyttāy oṉṟiṭiyiṭṭu niṉṟa mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mutal cārpaṉittumāya āviyum oṟṟum mēṟkūṟiya aḷaviṟantu mikkolittaliyum poruntum, iciviḷi paṇṭamāṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ e-ṟu.

ātiyeṉṟamiyāṉ, nāval kuṟippici muṟiyīṭu pulampaluṅ koḷka. iciyiṉ aḷaviṟanticikkuṅkāl āvi paṉṉiraṇṭumāttiri īṟākavum oṟṟup patiṉorumāttiriyīṟākavum icikku meṉṟār icinūlār; itu piṟanūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭaṉ paṭutaleṉṉum utti. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṉ aḷaviṟanticikkuṅkāl ulakanaṭi piṟaḻāticippatē variyaṟiyeṉka.

nāvaleṉpatu neṟpōr teḻikkum pakaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷit turappatoru col;
"kāva luḻavar kaṭuṅkaḷattup pōrēṟi
nāvalōooo veṉṟicikku nāḷōti - māvalavaṉ
kolyāṉi mēliruntu kūṟṟicittāṟ pōlumē
nalyāṉik kōkkiḷḷi nāṭu" (muttoḷḷāyiram)

eṉpataṉālaṟika.
"kaḵḵḵ ṟeṉṉuṅ kallata rattam",
"cuḵḵḵ ṟeṉṉun taṇṭōṭṭup peṇṇi"

eṉpaṉa kuṟippici: ēṉiya poruḷtōṉṟikkiṭattaliṉ aḷaviṟanticittal vantavaḻik kāṇka.

7. mutaṉili

NA_ca_102

paṉṉī ruyiruṅ kacatana pamavaya
ñaṅavī rintuyir meyyu moḻimutal.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē moḻikkumutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) paṉṉiraṇṭuyirum uyirūrnta ippattu meyyum moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) aṭi, āṭi, iṭi, īṭu, uṭi, ūṭal, eṭu, ēṭu, aiyam, oti, ōti, auviyam eṉavum, kaḷi, cavaṭi, taḷir, nalam, paṭi, mali, vaḷam yavaṉar, ñamali, aṅṅaṉam eṉavum varum; piṟavu maṉṉa.

uyirpōl taṉittu mutalāntaṉmiya allavātaliṉ uyir meyyeṉṟār. iḵtu iṉṉatalla tituveṉamoḻita leṉṉu mutti.

NA_ca_103

uū oō alavoṭu vammutal.

e-ṉ, potuvitiyuṭ ciṟappuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) innāṉkumallāta eṭṭuyirōṭu vakaram moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) vaḷi, vāḷi, viḷi, vīḻi, veḷi, vēḷi, vikal, vuvu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_104

aā uū ōau yammutal.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) iv vāṟuyirōṭum yakaram moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) yavaṉar, yāṉi, yukam, yūki, yōkam, yuvaṉam eṉa varum.

mēli oṭuvi varuvittuk koḷka.

NA_ca_105

aā eovvo ṭāku ñammutal.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) innāluyirōṭum ñakaram moḻikku mutalākum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) ñamali, ñālam, ñekiḻi, ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_106

cuṭṭiyā ekara viṉāvaḻi avvi
oṭṭi ṅacvu mutalā kummē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟucuṭṭum yāviṉāvum ekaraviṉāvumāya iṭiccoṟkaḷiṉpiṉ akarattiyoṭṭi ṅavvum moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam, uṅṅaṉam, yāṅṅaṉam, eṅṅaṉam eṉa varum.

ivaṟṟi aṅku, eṅku eṉṟāṟpōla orumoḻikaḷeṉṟāl eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, aññāṉṟu eṉpaṉapōlap piḷavupaṭṭu iṭiyē melloṟṟu mikkuvarutaliṉ toṭarmoḻikaḷēyāmeṉka; ṅaṉameṉpatu iṭattiṉiyum taṉmiyiṉiyumuṇarttum palaporuḷorucollāy variṉum taṉittu varuntaṉmiyataṉṟi muṭavaṉ kōlūṉṟivantāṟpōlac cuṭṭu viṉāvākiya iṭiccoṟkaḷi muṉṉiṭṭu varutalāṉ vaḻiyeṉṟum, ēṉiya meykaḷpōla mutalākāmiyiṉ avvōṭeṉṉātu oṭṭiyeṉṟum, oruvāṟṟāṉ mutalātaliṉ iḻivu ciṟappāka ṅavvu meṉṟum kūṟiṉār. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟalāṉ ṅakaram moḻikku mutalākāteṉpārkku uṭaṉpaṭalum maṟuttalumāyp piṟartam mata mēṟkoṇṭu kaḷivēyeṉṉum matampaṭak kūṟiṉāreṉṟuṇarka.

iṉi mutalāmeṉṟa pattuyirmeyyuḷ vakaramutaliya nāṉkiṉiyum vitantukūṟavē oḻinta āṟu meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭuyirōṭum mutalā meṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ peṟṟām.

8. īṟṟunili

NA_ca_107

āvi ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷamey
cāyu mukara nālāṟu mīṟē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē moḻikkīṟāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) taṉittum oṟṟōṭum varum āvimutalāka iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya irupattunāṉkeḻuttum moḻikku īṟām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, o, au eṉavum; cuṭṭum, viṉāvum uvamavurupaṭiyumāya ōreḻuttorumoḻiyām iṭiccollākiya a, i, u, e, o eṉavum; viḷa, palā, kari, kurī, kaṭu, marū, cē, ti, kō eṉavum; uriñ maṇ, porun maram, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vāḷ, aḵku eṉavum varum. kāṭṭātoḻintaṉa varuñ cūttirattāṟ kāṇka. kuṟṟukaramum uyirāy aṭaṅkumēṉum puṇarcci vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟa vēṟeṭuttōtiṉāreṉka.

NA_ca_108

kuṟṟuyi raḷapi ṉīṟām ekaram
meyyo ṭēlā tonnav voṭāmu
kakara vakaramo ṭāku meṉpa.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟuyirkku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuvitiyum ekarattiṟku eytiyatu orumaruṅkuvilakkalum okarattiṟkum aukārattiṟkum eytiyavaṟṟuṭ ciṟappuvitiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟuyir aḷapeṭiyiṉkaṇ varivaṭiviṉ aṟikuṟiyāyt taṉittu īṟām; ekaram meyyō ṭīṟākātu; okaram nakaramoṉṟuṭaṉ īṟām; aukāram kakara vakaraṅkaḷiraṇṭuṭaṉum īṟā meṉṟu colluvar pulavar. e-ṟu.

(u-m.)
"āa vaḷiya valavaṉ" eṉavum, no eṉavum, ku vu eṉavum varum.

neṭil aḷapeṭuppuḻi varivaṭivil aṟikuṟiyāyvaruṅ kuṟṟuyir neṭiloṭu toṭarntu uṭampaṭumeyyuṭaṉ īṟāntaṉmiyataṉṟit taṉittu niṟṟaliṟ kuṟṟuyiraḷapi ṉīṟāmeṉṟār. tāṉē īṟātalaṉṟik kuṟṟuyir aḷapiṉumīṟāmeṉa ummi koṭā toḻintār, uyirkaḻinta vuṭampi, "paṭumakaṉ kiṭikki kāṇūu" (puṟanā. 271) eṉṟār pōla, aṟikuṟi māttiriyāy niṉṟataṉi iṅṅaṉam kuṟṟuyireṉṟataṉṟi aḵtu ōreḻut taṉṟākaliṉ, ekara okara aukāraṅkaḷi vitantōtavē ēṉiyuyirkaḷ tamakkēṟṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāmeṉpatupeṟṟām.

mutalum cārpumāy vakuttu virittukkoṇṭē ellāveḻuttum uyirum meyyum āytamumāyaṭaṅkutaliṉ, mutalum īṟumākāta āytatti yoḻittu oḻinta uyirum meyyumāya iraṇṭiṉiyum uyirmutal, meymmutal, uyirīṟu, meyyīṟeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭār. mēlvarum puṇarcciviti aṭaṅkutaṟporuṭṭeṉak koḷka.

NA_ca_109

niṉṟa neṟiyē yuyirmey mutalīṟē.

e-ṉ, eḻuttiṉatu mutalum īṟumāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) uyirmeyyiṉatu mutal yātu, ataṉ īṟu yāteṉiṉ, oṟṟumuṉṉum uyir piṉṉumāy olittu niṉṟa neṟiyē mey ataṉ mutalām, uyir ataṉ īṟām e-ṟu.

eṉavē uyirum oṟṟum āyta mutaliya oṉpatu cārpeḻuttukkaḷum ōreḻuttātaliṉ. avaṟṟiṟku mutalumīṟum aviyēyāmeṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. iḵtu eñciya colli ṉeytakkūṟa leṉṉu mutti. it toṭakkatta vallaṉa colliṉ muṭiviṉapporuḷ muṭitta leṉṉu mutti. muṉṉar moḻikku mutalīṟeṉṟavaṟṟuḷḷum piṉṉar iṭinili pōlipatam puṇarpeṉpavaṟṟuḷḷum uyirmeyyip pirittut taṉi meyyum taṉiyuyirumpōla vittu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟaliṉ, moḻikku mutalum īṟuṅ kūṟutalaṉṟi eḻuttiṟku mutalumīṟum kūṟiṉāreṉṟuṇarka. ic cūttiram ciṅka nōkkam: aṟṟaṉṟi āṟṟoḻukkāy muṉṉiṉṟa moḻimutalīṟurikkum cūttiraṅkaḷiyē toṭarntuvanta teṉpārumuḷar; piṉ, "ṅammuṉkavvām" (cū. 111 eṉṟum, "iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉu niṉṟa vuyirmuṉ, kacatapa mikum" (cū. 165) eṉṟum uyirmeyyiṉ uyiriyum meyyiyum vēṟupirittōtuvatu ilakkaṇamaṉṟāy muṭiyumātaliṉ, atu poruntāteṉka.

eṇṇatikārattuḷ uyirmeyyi ōreḻuttāka eṇṇiṉār īṇṭu iraṇṭākavittu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuva teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, appoṭu peyta uppē pōla uyiroṭu puṇarttiya mey taṉṉaḷavu tōṉṟātu oṉṟāyniṟṟaliṉ oṉṟumāy, oṟṟu muṉṉum uyir piṉṉumāy olittu niṉṟa neṟiyāṉ iraṇṭumāy niṟṟaliṉeṉṟuṇarka. ivvāṟṟāṉ uyirmeyyeṉṉuñ col māttirivakiyāṉ ummit tokippuṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokiyākavum, olivakiyāṉ ummit tokiyākavum koḷḷappaṭum.

9. iṭinili meymayakkam

NA_ca_110

kacatapa voḻitta ḷirēḻaṉ kūṭṭam
meymmayak kuṭaṉili raḻavoḻit tīreṭ
ṭākumiv virupāṉ mayakku moḻiyiṭi
mēvu muyirmey mayakkaḷa viṉṟē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē moḻikku iṭiniliyāmāṟu uṇarttuṅkāl uyiruṭaṉ uyirmayaṅkalum meyyuṭaṉ meymayaṅkalum ivviraṇṭum māṟi mayaṅkalumeṉa mūṉṟāka vakukkappaṭum: ivaṟṟuḷ uyiruṭaṉuyirkku mayaṅkum taṉmi iṉmiyiṉ ataṉiyoḻittu ēṉi yiraṇṭiṉiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) meypatiṉeṭṭaṉuḷ, kacatapa veṉṉu nāṉkiyumoḻitta patiṉāṉku meyyum piṟameykaḷōṭu kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam vēṟṟunili meymayakkamām; rakāra ḻakāra meṉṉu miraṇṭiṉiyu moḻittu oḻinta patiṉāṟu meyyum tammoṭu kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam uṭaṉili meymayakkamām; ivviraṇṭu pakuti mayakkamum moḻikku iṭiyē varum; ivvāṟu meyyuṭaṉ mey mayaṅkutalaṉṟi uyiruṭaṉ meyyum meyyuṭaṉ uyirum māṟiuyiru meyyum mayaṅku mayakkattiṟku variyaṟi yiṉṟu; vēṇṭiyavāṟē mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

kūṭṭameṉiṉum mayakkameṉiṉum okkum. uṭaṉili yeṉṟataṉāṟ piṟameyyeṉpatu peṟṟām. moḻiyeṉṟatu orumoḻi toṭarmoḻi yeṉṉum iraṇṭiṉiyum. uyirum meyyum mayaṅkutaṟku iṭam vitantukūṟātu potuppaṭak kūṟiṉār. moḻiyiṉum uyirmeyyiṉum ōreḻuttiṉum varutaliṉ.

iccūttirattāṉ meyyuṭaṉ mey mayaṅkuṅkāṟ kacatapa veṉṉum nāṉkum tammoṭu tāmē mayaṅkumeṉavum, raḻaveṉṉum iraṇṭum tammoṭu piṟavē mayaṅkumeṉavum oḻintapaṉṉiraṇṭu meyyum tammoṭu tāmum piṟavum mayaṅkum eṉavum peṟṟām.

uyirum meyyum mayaṅkutaṟku utāraṇam: al vil eṉavum, kā eṉavum moḻikkaṇ mayaṅkiṉa; ka eṉa eḻuttiṉkaṇ mayaṅkiṉa. meyyuṭaṉ mey mayaṅkutaṟku utāraṇam mēlvarum ciṟappuc cūttirattiṟ kāṇka.

/ivviṭiniliyi orumoḻi toṭarmoḻi yiraṇṭiṉuṅ koḷḷātu orumoḻikkēyeṉak koḷvāru muḷar. evviṭattu variṉum iraṇṭeḻuttu iṇaṅkip poruntum poruttuvāyi iṭiniliyeṉ ṟōtalāṉum, iṇaṅkātaṉavariṉ vikārappaṭutalāṉum atu nūṟkaruttaṉṟeṉka.

iṉi, uyirmeymayakkaḷaviṉṟē yeṉpataṟku uyirmey muṉ uyirmey mayaṅkutal variyaṟiyiṉṟeṉap poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar; iṅṅaṉam uyirmeyyi iraṇṭeḻuttākap pirittu mayakkaviti kūṟuvār. ataṉiyē oṉṟāka vittu mayakkaviti kūṟārātalāṉum kūṟiṉum iṭinilimayakkamuḻuvatum iccūttirattu aṭaṅkāmiyāṉum atu poruntāteṉka.

((meymmayakkam))

NA_ca_111

ṅammuṉ kavvām vammuṉ yavvē.

e-ṉ, vēṟṟunili meymmayakkattuḷ vitantu ciṟappuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ṅakaramuṉṉark kakaramum vakaramuṉṉar yakaramum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kaṅkaṉ, tevyātu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_112

ñanamuṉ ṟammiṉam yakaramo ṭākum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭumey muṉṉarum tamakkiṉamākiya cakara takaraṅkaḷum yakaramum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kañcaṉ, uriñyātu; kantaṉ, porunyātu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_113

ṭaṟamuṉ kacapa meyyuṭaṉ mayaṅkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭu meymmuṉṉarum kacapa veṉṉum mūṉṟumeyyum iṇaṅki mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kaṭkam, kaṭci, nuṭpam; kaṟka, kaṟciṟār, kaṟpa eṉavarum.

NA_ca_114

ṇaṉamuṉ ṉiṉaṅkaca ñapamaya vavvarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭu meymmuṉṉarum tamakkiṉamākiya ṭaṟakkaḷum kacañapamayava eṉṉum ēḻu meyyum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) viṇṭu, veṇkalam: veṇcōṟu, veṇñamali, veṇpal, veṇmalar, maṇyātu, maṇvalitu eṉavum, puṉṟali, puṉkaṇ, puṉcey, puṉñamali, puṉpayir, puṉmalar, poṉyātu, poṉvalitu eṉavum varum.

NA_ca_115

mammuṉ payava mayaṅku meṉpa.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.0 makara meymmuṉṉarp pakara yakara vakaraṅkaḷ mayaṅkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kampaṉ kalamyātu kalam valitu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_116

yaraḻa muṉṉar moḻimutaṉ meyvarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) immūṉṟu meymmuṉṉarum moḻimutaṟku vitantu koṇṭa pattumeyyum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) vēy vēr vīḻ; kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, ñāṉṟatu, yātu, valitu eṉavum; vēyṅkuḻal, ārṅkōṭu pāḻṅkiṇaṟu eṉavum varum.

curuṅkac collaleṉṉum vaṉappuvakiyāṉ moḻimutaṉ meyyeṉak kūṟaliṉ yakarattiṉmuṉ yakaramayaṅkumuṭaṉili īṇṭuk koḷḷātoḻika.

NA_ca_117

laḷamuṉ kacapa vayavoṉ ṟummē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭu meymmuṉṉarum, kacapavaya eṉṉum aintu meyyum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) vēl, vāḷ; kaṭitu, ciṟitu, peritu, valitu, yātu eṉa varum.

((uṭaṉili mayakkam))

NA_ca_118

raḻaval laṉatammuṟ ṟāmuṭa ṉiliyum.

e-ṉ, uṭaṉilimeymmayakkattiṉ ciṟappuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) rakāra ḻakāramallaṉavākiya vēṟṟunili meymmayakkattuṭ kūṟiya paṉṉiraṇṭu meyyum uṭaṉilikkē uriya kacatapa veṉṉu nāṉkum tammiṟṟām uṭaṉiṉṟu mayaṅkum e-ṟu. (u-m.) aṅṅaṉam, avvi, aññāṉam, annīr, aṭṭu, aṟṟam, aṉṉi, aṇṇam, ammi, ayyam, alli, aḷḷal eṉavum; akku, accu, attu, appu eṉavum varum.

NA_ca_119

yaraḻavoṟ ṟiṉmuṉ kacatapa ṅañanama
īroṟ ṟāmraḻat taṉikkuṟi laṇiyā.

e-ṉ, taṉimeyyuṭaṉ taṉimeyyāyum mayaṅkuvaṉa iṉiyaveṉpatūum, moḻikku uṟuppāka mayaṅkātaṉa iṉiyaveṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) yaraḻa veṉṉu mūṉṟoṟṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ mayaṅkumeṉa vititta pattoṟṟuḷ iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya eṭṭoṟṟum taṉimeyyum uyirūrnta meyyumāy mayaṅkuvaṉavaṉṟi īroṟṟāyum mayaṅkuvaṉavām; rakāra ḻakāra voṟṟum taṉikkuṟilum mayaṅkun taṉmiyavāyiṉum moḻikkuṟuppāka varuvatum niṟpatumāy mayaṅkā e-ṟu.

īroṟṟumāmeṉṉum ummiyum moḻikku uṟuppākaveṉṉum coṟkaḷum eñci niṉṟaṉa.

(u-m.) vēykkuṟi, vērkkuṟi, vīḻkkuṟi; ciṟi, tali, puṟam eṉavum, vēyṅkuḻal, ārṅkōṭu, pāḻṅkiṇaṟu; kāyntaṉam, nērntaṉam, vāḻntaṉam eṉavum varum.

ēṉiya vantavaḻik kāṇka.

aṇiyā veṉṟa ākātaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇaṅkāṭṭalākāmi yuṇarka.

NA_ca_120

laḷamey tirinta ṉaṇamuṉ makāram
nintī roṟṟāñ ceyyu ḷuḷḷē.

e-ṉ, ceyyuṭkuriyavāy īroṟṟuṭaṉiṟpaṉa iṉiyaveṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) lakāra ḷakāra voṟṟuttirinta ṉakāra ṇakāra muṉvarum makara voṟṟu muṉṉarkkūṟiyavāṟē kuṟuki avaṟṟuṭaṉ īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyām, ceyyuḷakattu e-ṟu.

(u-m.)
"ticiyaṟi, mīkāṉum pōṉm" (pari. 10. 55) eṉavum
"mayiliyaṉ mātar maruṇm" eṉavum varum.

makāraninteṉa aṉuvatittār, makaraṅkuṟukutaṟkut toṭarum ṉakaraṇakaraṅkaḷ lakaraḷakaran tirintaṉaveṉpatūum akkuṟukkam ceyyuṭkaṇṇateṉpatūum uyttuṇartaṟkeṉka.

NA_ca_121

tampeyar moḻiyiṉ mutalu mayakkamum
immuṟi māṟiyu miyalu meṉpa.

e-ṉ, moḻimutal īṟu iṭinilikaṭku āvatoru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) eḻuttukkaḷ tampeyarkaḷi moḻintu nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇarkkumiṭattu, ellā moḻi mutaṟkum iṅṅaṉam vitittaṉavum vilakkiyaṉavumākiya ellā veḻuttum mutalām; mayakkattiṟku iṅṅaṉam vitittaṉavum vilakkiyaṉavumākiya ellā veḻuttum mayaṅkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

iṅṅaṉam ellāveḻuttum taṉittaṉi ataṟkatuvē mutalāka varumeṉavē, īṟṟiṟku vitittaṉavum vilakkiyaṉavumākiya ellāveḻuttum taṉittaṉi ataṟkatuvē īṟāka varumeṉac collātē aruttāpattiyāṉ amitaliṉ īṟumeṉa mikipaṭak kūṟātoḻintār.

(u-m.) "avaṟṟuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum" (tol. nūṉ. cū. 24) eṉpuḻi lakaram mutalākiyum kūṉiṟuti ḷakara voṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiyum niṉṟatu. cepperitu eṉpuḻi ekaram meyyō ṭīṟāy niṉṟatu. piṟavum variyaṟiyiṉṟi varutal kāṇka.

āytattiṟkup piṟappatikārattuḷ varuvāy kūṟiyavaḻi mayakkaviti amintu kiṭantamiyāṉum taṉpeyar moḻiya vārāmiyāṉum kūṟātoḻintār.

((10. pōli))

((moḻiyiṟutippōli))

NA_ca_122

makara viṟuti yaḵṟiṇip peyariṉ
ṉakaramō ṭuṟaḻā naṭappaṉa vuḷavē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē pōliyāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉ toṭaṅki moḻiyiṟutippōli uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pālpakā aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiṉiṭattu iṟuti makaram ṉakarattōṭu ottu naṭappaṉa uḷavām e-ṟu.

makaraviṟuti eṉṟataṉāṟ pālpakāvaḵṟiṇip peyareṉpatu peṟṟām. makaram ṉakarattōṭottalāvatu peyariṟutikkaṇ makaram niṉṟa nilikkaḷattu ṉakaram niṟpiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉṟiyottal.

(u-m.)
"akaṉamarntu ceyyā ḷuṟiyu mukaṉamarntu
nalvirun tōmpuvā ṉil" (kuṟaḷ, 84.)

itaṉuḷ, akam - akaṉ, mukam - mukaṉ eṉavum; talam - talaṉ, nilam - nilaṉ, kalam - kalaṉ, nalam - nalaṉ, valam - valaṉ eṉavum varutal kāṇka. piṟavumaṉṉa.

uṟaḻānaṭappaṉavuḷa veṉṉuñ collāṟṟalāṉ uṟaḻātaṉa perumpāla veṉka. avi: vaṭṭam. kuṭṭam, māṭam, kūṭam mutalāyiṉa. pālpakāvaḵṟiṇippeyariṭattu iyalpāya makaraviṟutiniliyiṉ ṉakaram pōliyāy varumeṉavē akaṉ mukaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷip pālpakutta āṭūuvaṟi colleṉak koḷḷaṟka eṉpatūum, ekiṉ, payiṉ, kuyiṉ, vayiṉ mutaliya ṉakaravīṟṟuppeyarkku ivviyappāṭilli yeṉpatūum peṟṟām.

iṉi, ivviṟutippōliyi muṟkūṟiyataṉāl, curumpu - curumpar, arumpu - arumpar, cāmpal-cāmpar, pantal - pantar mutaliyaṉavum koḷka.

(moḻi mutaṟpōliyum moḻiyiṭippōliyum)

NA_ca_123

aai mutaliṭi yokkuñ cañayamuṉ.

e-ṉ, moḻimutaṟ pōliyum moḻiyiṭip pōliyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) moḻimutaliṉum iṭiyiṉum akara aikāraṅkaḷ vēṟṟumi yiṉṟi ottu naṭappaṉavām, cakara ñakara yakaraṅkaṭku muṉṉar varumāyiṉ e-ṟu.

īṇṭu muṉṉeṉṟatu, kālamuṉṉeṉak koḷka.

(u-m.) pacal - pical (piyal), mañcu - miñcu, mayal - miyal eṉavum; amaccu - amiccu, ilañci - iliñci, arayar - ariyar eṉavum varum. piṟavumaṉṉa.

((moḻiyiṭippōli))

NA_ca_124

aikāṉ yavvaḻi navvoṭu cilvaḻi
ñaḵkā ṉuṟaḻu meṉmaru muḷarē.

e-ṉ, ituvum moḻiyiṭippōli uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) moḻiyiṭikkaṭ cilaviṭattu aikārattiṉ piṉṉum yakaravoṟṟiṉpiṉṉum iyalpāyvarum nakarattōṭu ñakaram uṟaḻu meṉṟu colvārumuḷar e-ṟu.

ippōli arukivarutaliṟ cilvaḻiyeṉṟum, ellārkkum oppa muṭintataṉṟākaliṉ eṉmarumeṉṟum kūṟiṉār.

(u-m.)
"ceyññiṉṟa nīla malarkiṉṟa tillicciṟ ṟampalavaṉ
miññiṉṟa voṇkaṇ malimakaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻntuniṟka
neyaññiṉ ṟeriyum viḷakkotta nīla maṇimiṭaṟṟāṉ
kiññiṉṟa vāṭalkaṇṭāṟ piṉṉik kaṇkoṇṭu kāṇpateṉṉē"

(tē. kōyil, tirunā, tiruviruttam "pāḷiyuṭi", 5) eṉavum, aiññūṟu eṉavum varum.

iccūttiram piṟaṉkōṭkūṟa leṉṉu mutti.
"maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē"
maṇñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē" eṉavum
"poṉyātta tārpuravi parikkumē"
"poṉñātta tārpuravi parikkumē"

eṉavum varumoḻi viṉikkumuṉṉar yā niṉṟa nilikkaḷattu ñā niṟpiṉu mokkumpōlikku āciriyar tolkāppiyar,
"ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
viṉiyō raṉiya veṉmaṉār pulavar" (toki. cū. 4)

eṉac cūttirañ ceytavāṟu ivvāciriyarum cūttirañ ceyyā toḻital iṟantatuvilakkaleṉṉum utti. piṟavu maṉṉa.

immūṉṟu cūttirattāṉuṅkūṟiya pōlikaḷi iṭinili mayakkattiṉ pāṟpaṭuttip poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar; iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya eḻuttukkaḷ moḻikkuṟuppāki oṉṟu niṉṟa nilikkaḷattu maṟṟoṉṟu atupōla moḻinirappa niṟpataṉṟi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mayaṅki iraṇṭeḻuttum uṭaṉiṟpatiṉmiyāṉum, mutalīṟiṭinilikaṭkup puṟaṉaṭiyuṅkūṟik kuṟi vaṟuttamiyāṉum, `uṟaḻānaṭappaṉa' eṉṟum `okkum' eṉṟum, `uṟaḻum' eṉṟum uvamavurupu koṭuttu immūṉṟu cūttiramum kūṟutalāṉum atu poruntāteṉka.

((eḻuttuppōli))

NA_ca_125

ammuṉ ikaram yakara meṉṟivi
eytiṉ aiyot ticikkum avvō
ṭuvvum vavvum auvō raṉṉa.

e-ṉ, eḻuttuppōli uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) akarattiṉmuṉṉar ikaramum yakaravoṟṟum varumāyiṉ aikāram pōṉṟolikkum; akarattōṭu ukaramum vakaravoṟṟum varumāyiṉ aukāram pōṉṟolikkum e-ṟu.

uyirōṭuyirkku mayakkamiṉmiyiṉ iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya kuṟṟuyirkaḷi valintu mayakkikkūṟiṉ. aikāra aukārampōl icittalāṉum kuṟṟoṟṟukkaḷ oṉṟarimāttiriyavātaliṉ iraṇṭu māttiriyavākiya aikāra aukāraṅkaḷōṭu ovvāmiyiṉ oli vakiyāṉ mayakkik kūṟiṉ avi otticittalāṉum avaṟṟuḷḷum vakaram piṟappu vēṟupāṭṭāṉ oliyum orupuṭiyottalāṉum uytiṉeṉṟār.

(u-m.) ai = ai ay = ai ki = ki, kay = ki au = au, av = au, ku = ku eṉavum,
"kayvēl kaḷiṟṟoṭu pōkki varupavaṉ
meyvēl paṟiyā nakum" (kuṟaḷ, 774) eṉavum,
"avviya neñcattā ṉākkamuñ cevviyāṉ
kēṭu niṉikkap paṭum" (kuṟaḷ, 169) eṉavum varum.

icceyyuṭkaṇ toṭinōkki aikāra aukāram niṉṟa nilikkaḷattap pōli vantavāṟu kāṇka.

((eḻuttiṉ cāriyi))

NA_ca_126

meykaḷ akaramu neṭṭuyir kāramum
aiauk kāṉu mirumik kuṟiliv
viraṇṭoṭu karamumāñ cāriyi peṟumpiṟa.

e-ṉ, mēl eḻuttilakkaṇaṅkūṟiya mukattāṟ pōnta eḻuttiṉ cāriyikaḷit tokuttu iṉamaṭittal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) meykaḷ akaraccāriyiyum, neṭṭuyirkaḷ kārac cāriyiyum, avaṟṟuḷ aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ kāraccāriyiyuṭaṉē kāṉcāriyiyum, uyirkkuṟilum uyirmeykkuṟilum kāramkāṉeṉṉum ivviraṇṭuṭaṉē karaccāriyiyum peṟum e-ṟu.

meykaḷi muṟkūṟiṉār, avaṟṟiṟku akaraccāriyi iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉ. meykaḷ avvumeṉṉātu akaramu meṉṟār, aṅṅaṉam karam kāram kāṉkaḷ variṟ cāriyi akarattiṉatu cāriyiyeṉṟukōṭaṟku, āñcāriyiyeṉṟār, kāra mutaliya cāriyikaḷi ēṟṟapeṟṟi kōṭaṟku.

(u-m.) ka ṅa eṉavum, ākāram aikāram aukāram eṉavum, aikāṉ aukāṉ eṉavum, akāram aḵkāṉ akaram makāram maḵkāṉ makaram eṉavum varum. piṟavu maṉṉa.

piṟa veṉṟamiyāṟ kuṟiloṭu kāṉpuṇaruṅkāl iṭiyē āytattōṟṟamum vaḻaṅkum. piṟacāriyikaḷum koḷka. uyirmey neṭilum āytamum kūṟātoḻintār, uyirmey neṭil iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya mūṉṟucāriyiyum ēlāmiyāṉum āytam piṟavaḻi vārāmiyiṉ moḻiyiṭippaṭuttu ammoḻiyiṟutikkaṭ piṟacāriyitantu uyirvarukkattu iṟutikkaṇṇē vaḻaṅkutalāṉumeṉka.

vikaṟpattiṉmuṭittaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ mēl virintu muṭintu kiṭantaṉavaṟṟip pulappaṭavēṇṭi īṇṭuttokuttu muṭittalāṉ iccūttiram muṭintatu muṭittaleṉṉum utti.

((puṟaṉaṭi))

NA_ca_127

moḻiyāyt toṭariṉu muṉṉaṉit teḻuttē.

e-ṉ, ivviyalukku āvatoru puṟaṉaṭi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ivveḻuttukkaḷ patamāyiṉum appatam tammoṭum urupoṭum puṇariṉum avaṟṟāṟ piṟaḻātu muṉṉark kūṟiya appattilakkaṇattiyum uṭiyavām e-ṟu.

eḻuttu, cuṇṇattiṉkaṇ aricaṉa mutaliya pōlātu māliyiṉkaṇ malarpōla avaṟṟil niṟṟaliṉ muṉṉaṉit teṉṟār. eḻutteṉpatu cātiyorumiyātaliṉ, avaṉitteṉṉum orumiyoṭu muṭintatu. muṉṉaṉittātal pataviyal mutaliya nāṉkiyaluḷḷuṅ kāṇka.

((eḻuttiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((iraṇṭāvatu pataviyal))

((patam))

NA_ca_128

eḻuttē taṉittun toṭarntum poruṭariṟ
patamā matupakāp patampaku patameṉa
irupā lāki yiyalu meṉpa.

e-ṉ, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē patattiṉatu ilakkaṇam uṇarttuvāṉ toṭaṅki eḻuttāṟ patamātalum, aḵtu ittuṇiyateṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) eḻuttukkaḷ tāmē ovvoṉṟu taṉittum iraṇṭu mutalākat toṭarntum piṟaporuḷit tarumāyiṉ atu patamām; appatam papakāppatameṉavum pakupatameṉavum iraṇṭu pakutiyatāki naṭakkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

eḻuttāṉāya patam aricaṉa mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ camittacuṇṇam pōlātu malarāṟ camitta mālipōl niṟṟaliṉ eḻuttē yeṉṟum, ōreḻuttēṉum piṟaporuḷ tarutaliṟ patamāmeṉpār taṉitteṉṟum, oṉṟu iraṇṭu palaveṉpaṉa vaṭanūl vaḻakku, oṉṟallaṉa vellām palaveṉpatu tamiḻ naṭiyātaliṉ vaṭanūl vaḻakkuppaṟṟi āciriyar tolkāppiyar,
"ōreḻut torumoḻi yīreḻut torumoḻi
iraṇṭiṟan ticikkun toṭarmoḻi yuḷappaṭa
mūṉṟē moḻinili tōṉṟiya neṟiyē" (moḻi. cū. 12)

eṉṟārkku iraṇṭeḻuttut toṭarntaṉa vallaveṉiṉ atu moḻiyumaṉṟeṉpār taṉinili yoḻintavaṟṟit toṭarnteṉṟum, iṟiñi miṟiñiyeṉiṟ ṟoṭarumēṉum taṉṉiyuṇarttu maṉṟippiṟaporuḷ tārāmiyiṟ patamākāteṉpār poruḷ tariṉeṉṟum.
"taṉṉi yuṇartti ṉeḻuttām piṟaporuḷic
cuṭṭutaṟ kaṇṇēyāñ col"

eṉpavākaliṟ poruḷeṉṟatu piṟaporuḷeṉpār uṇarttiṉeṉṉātu tariṉeṉṟum, aṇuveṉṉum olinuṭpattāṉ eḻuttāṉāṟpōla (cū. 58) eḻutteṉṉum oli nuṭpattāṉ moḻiyāmeṉpār patamāmeṉṟum, ippataviyalukku mēṟkoḷ āriyamē yeṉpār moḻiyeṉṉātu patameṉṟum kūṟiṉār.

ic cūttirattuḷ toṭarntumeṉṟatu piṟarnūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭaleṉṉum matam.

((ōreḻuttorumoḻi))

NA_ca_129

uyirmavi lāṟun tapanavi lintum
kavacavi ṉālum yavvi loṉṟum
āku neṭiṉotu vāṅkuṟi liraṇṭō
ṭōreḻut tiyalpata māṟēḻ ciṟappiṉa.

e-ṉ, mēl eḻuttut taṉittum toṭarntum orumoḻiyāmeṉṟār, avaṟṟuḷ ōreḻuttorumoḻi ivi eṉpatūum, ittuṇiya veṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) uyir varukkattum mavvarukkattum avvāṟum, tavvarukkattum pavvarukkattum navvarukkattum aivintum, kavvarukkattum vavvarukkattum cavvarukkattum nannāṉkum, yavvarukkattu oṉṟum ākam neṭṭeḻuttāṉākiya moḻi nāṟpatum: novvum tuvvumākum kuṟṟeḻuttāṉākiya moḻi iraṇṭuṭaṉē ōreḻuttāṉākiya moḻi innāṟpattiraṇṭum ciṟappiṉavām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) ā ī ū ē ai ō e-m, mā mī mū mē mi mō e-m. tā tī tū tē ti e-m, pā pū pē pi pō e-m, nā nī nē nō e-m, kā kū ki kō e-m, vā vī vi vu e-m, cā cī cē cō e-m, yā e-m, no tu e-m varum.

ivaṟṟuḷ ū - iṟicci; ō - matakunīr tāṅkum palaki: pē - nuri; nē - aṉpu; cō - araṇ; no tu eṉṉum kuṟiliraṇṭum tuṉpi puci eṉṉum ēval.

ivi ciṟappiṉaveṉavē vakaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarp poruḷi oppumiyāṉ uṇarttiniṟṟalāṉ au eṉṉum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyum cuṭṭu viṉā uvamipporuḷit tarum iṭiccollātalāṟ kuṟṟuyirintāṉāya ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷum ku veṉṉum uyirmeyyāṉāya ōreḻuttoru moḻiyum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum ciṟappillaṉaveṉak koḷka.

((toṭareḻuttorumoḻi))

NA_ca_130

pakāppata mēḻum pakupata moṉpatum
eḻuttī ṟākat toṭaru meṉpa.

e-ṉ, toṭareḻuttorumoḻiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pakāppatam iraṇṭeḻuttu mutal ēḻeḻuttīṟākavum, pakupatam iraṇṭeḻuttu mutal oṉpateḻuttīṟākavum toṭarumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

(u-m.) aṇi, aṟam, akalam, aruppam, taruppaṇam, uttiraṭṭāti e-m: kūṉi, kūṉaṉ, kuḻiyaṉ, poruppaṉ, ampalavaṉ, araṅkattāṉ, uttirāṭattāṉ, uttiraṭṭātiyāṉ e-m varum. eḻuntirukkiṉṟār, naṭattuvippikkiṟār, naṭattuvippikkiṟārkaḷ, naṭattuvippikkiṉṟārkaḷ, naṭattuvippikkiṉṟavarkaḷeṉap pakuti mutaliya uṟuppu vēṟupaṭṭu varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku ivvariyaṟi illiyeṉka.

((pakāppatam))

NA_ca_131

pakuppāṟ payaṉaṟ ṟiṭukuṟi yāki
muṉṉē yoṉṟāy muṭintiyal kiṉṟa
peyarviṉi yiṭiyuri nāṉkum pakāppatam.

e-ṉ, mēṟpatam pakāppatam pakupatameṉa iraṇṭu pakutiyatāy naṭakkumeṉṟār, avaṟṟuṭ pakāppatam iṉiyaveṉpatūum, ittuṇiyaveṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pakupatampōl itaṉip pakuttup pārkkiṉ appakuppāṟ pakuti vikuti mutaliya uṟuppiṉ payaṉviḷivatiṉṟik kāraṇattāṉ varum appatampōlātu iṭukuṟiyāyp pakuti vikuti mutaliya āṟuṟuppāṉum piṉpu muṭikkappaṭum appatam pōlātu paṭippukkālantoṭṭu oṉṟāy muṭintu naṭakkiṉṟa peyarccol, viṉiccol, iṭiccol, uriccol eṉṉum nāṉkum pakāppatamām e-ṟu.

pakupatattiṟku maṟutalippaṭṭateṉpār ivvāṟu kūṟiṉār.

(u-m.) nilam, nīr eṉavum, naṭa vā eṉavum, naṭa vā eṉavum, maṉ kol eṉavum uṟu kaḻi eṉavum varum. piṟavumaṉṉa.

((pakupatam))

NA_ca_132

poruḷiṭaṅ kālañ ciṉikuṇantoḻiliṉ
varupeyar poḻutukoḷ viṉipaku patamē.

e-ṉ, pakupatam iṉiyaveṉpatūum, ittuṇiya veṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya poruḷātiyāṟum kāraṇamāka varum peyarccoṟkaḷum, teriniliyāyum kuṟippāyum kālattik koḷḷum viṉiccoṟkaḷumākiya iraṇṭum pakupatamām e-ṟu.

(132_V1) poḻutukoḷviṉi pakupatameṉavē avviruvaki viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarum pakupatamāmeṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ kūṟiṉāreṉka. puḷi, kaṭu mutaliya ākupeyarum kāraṇattāṉ varumēṉum vikuti mutaliya uṟuppiṉmiyiṉ aḵtoḻintaṉavaṟṟiyē varupeyareṉṟum, naṭa vā mutaliya orumiyēval poḻutukoḷ viṉiyāyiṉum pakutiyoṉṟumē poruḷāṉ vēṟupaṭṭataṉṟip pakuttaṟku vikuti mutaliya uṟuppiṉmiyiṟ pakāppatamēyaṉṟip pakupatamākāmiyiṉ aḵtoḻintaṉavaṟṟip poḻutukoḷviṉi yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāreṉka.
